巧新 (1563 TT)  Trading Buy

2H26 、 2027 營運展望向上，投資建議 Trading Buy 。  
投資建議 Trading Buy ：巧新 (1563 TT) 努力進行轉型，半導體
新事業有望於 2H26 取得進展，且公司穩定配發現金股利並將
進行減資。雖目前評價並未偏低，但考量 2H26 獲利動能向上，
2026 年獲利表現回升且 2027 年獲利能力續揚，投資建議
Trading Buy ，目標價 63.5 元 (以 2026 年 EPS 及 PE 23 倍評價 )。 
2Q26 本業獲利增長，但整體獲利下滑；預估 2H26 獲利動能向
上： 2Q26 營收 19.80 億元， QoQ+5.76% 、YoY+15.45% 。毛利率
由 1Q26 的 22.01% 提升至 24.32% ，受到運輸成本上漲影響，營
業費用同步增長，營業利益 1.95 億元， QoQ+19.28% 、
YoY+35.95% 。因業外認列大筆匯兌損失，業外淨收入 -1.06 億
元，稅率表現則維持穩定。受到業外損失影響，稅後純益 0.71
億元， QoQ-45.53% 、 YoY 轉虧為盈， EPS 0.32 元。 2H26 方面，
以車廠預示量來看，營收有望較 1H26 增長 5~8% 。由於再生鋁
採用率於 2Q26 中下旬大幅提升，實質貢獻營收將落於 3Q26 末
~4Q26，將帶動 2H26 毛利率增長。業外則有機會回沖匯兌損失。
整體來看， 2H26 獲利動能向上。  
預估 2026 年獲利表現回升， 2027 年獲利能力續揚： 2026 年方
面，輪圈出貨小幅提升，而半導體相關零組件預計自 2H26 開始
貢獻營收。預估 2026 營收 79.71 億元， YoY+14.24% 。隨著再生
鋁採用率提升且營收規模增長 ， 毛利率有望逐季提升 ， 預估2026
年毛利率提升至 24.91% ，營業利益 YoY+56.84% 。若業外較無
匯兌損失影響 ， 預估稅後純益 6.23 億元 ，YoY+95.83% ，EPS 2.76
元。 2027 年方面，根據車廠預示量，鋁圈出貨量將由 2026 年的
60 萬顆以上，提升至 70 萬顆以上，有助於帶動車用產品營收增
長。而半導體營收占比有機會提升至 5~10% ，將提供額外營收
動能。因再生鋁採用比重提升，且半導體產品毛利率相對較高，
2027 年毛利率及獲利能力有望優於 2026 年。

2 群益投顧 
  巧新 (1563 TT) ESG ：  
Environmental 環境保護： (1) 鋁下腳料 100% 回收，製成再生鋁棒，持續循環
再利用。 (2) 導入各式節能設備具體節能，已降低溫室氣體排放量。 (3) 屏東
廠自建太陽能光電。 Social 社會責任： (1) 獲得 ASI( 鋁業管理倡議 )績效標準
認證。 (2) 定期進行人權盡職調查與評估，流程和政策涵蓋重要的人權問題，
保障員工和活動參與者的安全。 (3) 響應地方政府公益活動。 Governance 公
司治理： (1) 風險管理依公司程序辦理，內控嚴謹，並設有專職稽核部門定期
查核。 (2) 將誠信經營納入新人教育訓練課程與人力資源政策中，設立明確
有效之獎懲及申訴機制。 (3) 每年定期發布 ESG 報告書。  
公司概況：  
巧新成立於 1994 年，為國內汽車鍛造輪圈大廠，主要產品包含鍛造鋁圈、
鋁材、其他 (主要為鍛造鋁懸吊系統元件 )，輪圈相關營收占比約 85%。巧新
主要客戶為全球豪華車廠，包含 Jaguar Land Rover( 英 )、 Lexus/Toyota( 日 )、
Porsche( 德 )、 BMW( 德 )、 Mercedes-Benz( 德 )。由於汽車供應鏈進入門檻高，
取得客戶認證需耗時 2~4 年，而從接單、驗證到量產出貨，需再花費 2~3 年
時間。若成為客戶主要供應商，將建立堅實的客戶合作關係。公司競爭利基
包含高強度輕量化、淨成形鍛造、再生鋁：  
 高強度輕量化方面，以同樣的輪圈尺寸相比，鍛造鋁圈較傳統鑄造鋁
圈能減少 25~30% 的重量。而在強度、耐用性、拉伸強度、抗衝擊性、
降低油耗、簧下重量、駕駛操控性上，鍛造鋁圈皆相對優異的表現。
缺點在於價格相對偏高，因此多用於豪華高端車款。由於鍛造鋁圈可
同時滿足電動車所需的高扭力加速、輕量化、操控與安全性，巧新積
極配合客戶開拓電動車市場。  
 淨成形鍛造方面，利用精密鍛造來縮短鍛造後的工序。鍛造過程中需
使用高精準的模具，並進行三道鍛次 (預成形、粗鍛、細鍛 )，但可減
少後續 CNC 機械加工需求。優點在於可降低生產成本、質量穩定，自
動化程度高適合大批量訂單。巧新淨成形鍛造鋁圈專案持續增加，目
前已有 30 款淨成形鍛造鋁圈量產中， 64 款開發中。  
 再生鋁方面，巧新回收生產過程中產生的 70% 下腳料，透過鋁屑添
加、輾碎篩選、脫水、烘乾加料、熔解及合金成分調整、澆鑄成型等
流程，製成再生鋁棒供鍛造使用。再生鋁相關產品強度與原生鋁相
同，但生產每噸鋁所產生的碳排放量減少 95% 。巧新的再生鋁獲得全
球知名車廠採用，已有 7 家車廠認證。由於再生鋁再製成本較原先鋁
購買成本低，有助於減少 10~15% 原料成本，但售價並無明顯差異，故
有助於帶動毛利率提升。再生鋁年產能約 4 萬噸，建置中的屏東熔煉
二廠再生鋁年產能約 10 萬噸，預計 2Q27 開始加入營運。巧新先前再
生鋁使用率約達 40% ，受到美伊戰爭影響，原生鋁供應受阻，
05/2026~06/2026 再生鋁使用率已拉升至 80% 。除此之外，巧新規劃再
生鋁產品將供非鍛造輪圈產品使用。相關應用包含半導體設備 (通過技
術門檻，已進入送樣認證階段，以真空腔體、蝕刻製程的耗材為主 )、
移動設備、重電設備及其他。先前目標在三年內將非乘用車鋁圈營收
貢獻由 15% 提升至 40% 以上，本次法說展望相對樂觀，認為 2028 年非
車用營收將有機會佔到 50% 。

3 群益投顧

2Q26 本業獲利增長，但受業外影響，整體獲利下滑；預估 2H26 獲利動能
向上：  
2Q26 方面，營收 19.80 億元， QoQ+5.76% 、 YoY+15.45% 。毛利率由 1Q26
的 22.01% 提升至 24.32% ，受到運輸成本上漲影響，營業費用同步增長，營
業利益 1.95 億元， QoQ+19.28% 、YoY+35.95% 。因業外認列大筆匯兌損失，
業外淨收入 -1.06 億元，稅率表現則維持穩定。受到業外損失影響，稅後純
益 0.71 億元， QoQ-45.53% 、 YoY 轉虧為盈， EPS 0.32 元。 2H26 方面，以
車廠預示量來看，營收有望較 1H26 增長 5~8% 。由於再生鋁採用率於 2Q26
中下旬大幅提升，實質貢獻營收將落於 3Q26 末 ~4Q26 ，將帶動 2H26 毛利
率增長。業外則有機會回沖匯兌損失。整體來看， 2H26 獲利動能向上。  
預估 2026 年獲利表現回升， 2027 年獲利能力續揚：  
雖全球車市成長動能平緩，並持續面臨中國車廠的競爭壓力；但因英國主要
客戶在資安事件過後拉貨動能回升，且巧新與客戶關係穩定，預估輪圈出貨
有望提升。半導體新事業方面，巧新鎖定高毛利的半導體先進製程前段設備
零組件，預計自 2H26 開始貢獻營收，雖 2026 年營收貢獻不到 1%，但 2027
年營收占比有機會提升至 5~10% 。此外，巧新透過再生鋁產品提升獲利能

4 群益投顧 
力，因美伊戰爭導致原生鋁供應受阻，有助於進一步提升客戶對於再生鋁的
採用意願， 2Q26 中下旬再生鋁採用率已大幅拉升。預估 2026 營收 79.71 億
元， YoY+14.24% 。隨著再生鋁採用率提升且營收規模增長，毛利率有望逐
季提升，預估 2026 年毛利率由 2025 年的 21.27% 提升至 24.91% ，營業利益
YoY+56.84% 。如果業外較無匯兌損失影響，預估稅後純益 6.23 億元，
YoY+95.83% ， EPS 2.76 元。  
2027 年方面，根據車廠預示量，鋁圈出貨量將由 2026 年的 60 萬顆以上，
提升至 70 萬顆以上，有助於帶動車用產品營收 增長。而半導體營收占比有
機會提升至 5~10% ，將提供額外營收動能。因再生鋁採用比重提升，且半導
體產品毛利率相對較高， 2027 年毛利率及獲利能力有望優於 2026 年。  
投資建議：  
巧新 (1563 TT) 努力進行轉型，半導體新事業有望於 2H26 取得進展，且公司
穩定配發現金股利並將進行減資。雖目前評價並未偏低，但考量 2H26 獲利
動能向上， 2026 年獲利表現回升且 2027 年獲利能力續揚，投資建議 Trading 
Buy ，目標價 63.5 元 (以 2026 年 EPS 及 PE 23 倍評價 )。

中立 (Neutral)  
無法由基本面給予投資評等  
預期近期股價將處於盤整  
建議降低持股

【投資評等調整機制】  
  投資評等係「首次」給予特定個股投資評等時，其前一日收盤價相較 3 個月
目標價之潛在上漲空間計算而得。個股投資評等分為四個等級，定義如上。
爾後的投資評等係依循「首次評等」，直到停止推薦。   
「強力買進」、「買進」及「區間操作」均有 upside 目標價。差別僅在於，
首次評等時潛在報酬率不同。「中立」則無目標價。  
一旦我們給予特定個股「強力買進」 、 「買進」或「區間操作」之投資評等，
就是責任的開始，爾後將透過各式研究報告作定期性、持續性追蹤基本面及
股價變化，直到停止推薦。  
停止推薦情境：  
1.  達目標價。  
2.  雖未達原訂目標價，但檢視基本面、訊息面、籌碼面等多方訊息，研
判股價上漲空間已然有限，將適時出具降評報告。  
3.  推薦後股價不漲反跌，亦將出具降評報告。

【免責聲明】  
  本研究報告僅提供予特定人之客戶作為參考資料「非經同意不得轉載」 。我
們並不確保此資訊的完整性與正確性，投資人應了解，報告中有關未來預測
之陳述可能不會實現，因而不應被依賴。而且此報告並非根據特定投資目的
或依預定對象之財務狀況所撰寫出來的，因此，此研究報告的目的，既非對
投資人於買賣證券、選擇權、期貨或其他證券相關之衍生性商品提供詢價服
務，亦非作為進行交易的要約。投資人應注意到相關證券之價值及收益，可
能會有無預警地上升或下降，產生投資回報金額可能比原始投資來得少的
情形。

全球 CSP Capex 持續上修，川湖提前 2 年擴產因應 
 全球 CSP 上修 Capex，加上川益三、五期新廠年底動工，產能增加 2
倍有餘，確認未來營運成長無虞，同時上 修獲利及目標價 ：投顧上修
2026、2027 年稅後 EPS 分別來到 393.72 元及 612.06 元，依目前股
價 11,110 元，PER 18X(2027EPS) 仍顯著落後競爭對手(高達 30X)。
法說已定調川益 3、5 期新廠建置規劃時程，將提前於今年底開始動
工，亦將確認今明年的營運成長無虞，且產能擴增 2 倍亦為未來獲利
提升想像空間，市場對川湖 2026、2027 年營運仍有再上修空間，依
其 2027 年獲利股價仍有再上修至 18,360 元的空間(30X 2027EPS) ，
故投顧維持買進投資評等 ， 目標價暫上修至15,300 元(25X 2027EPS)。 
 3Q26 受惠 AI 算力需求持續攀升，營運將再創高，2H26 年增率可期，
預估營收季增 80%，單季稅後 EPS 137.9 元：展望 3Q26，7 月營收
續創歷較新高，稼動率已>70~80%之上，公司未來 2~3 個月仍會持續
增加人力資源以補充產能， 滿足客戶需求。展望未來，川湖將持續加
速研發 2027、2028 和 2029 年的突破性產品。隨著人工智慧應用的不
斷加速，樂觀地認為，川湖客戶群分散幾百家，AI 算力需求增加，營
收只會愈來愈好，川湖市佔率高是主要受益者，2H26 將再創佳績，年
增 率 可 以 期 待 ， 能 見 度 很 高 。投顧 預估 3Q26 營收 194 億元 、
+80%QoQ、+346%Y oY，單季稅後 EPS 137.9 元。 
 展望 2026 年，2H26 能見度高，YoY 可以期待，加上毛利率中長期仍
是正面趨勢，CSP Capex 今明年 YoY 仍強勁成長，投顧上修今明年
稅後 EPS 至 393.72 元及 612.06 元：展望 2026 年，此次國際財報周，
北美四大 CSP Capex 持續上修至+108%Y oY來到 7,450 億美元；研
調機構 Trendforce 亦預估全球九大 CSP Capex +90%Y oY來到 8,867
億美元，同時預估 2027 年持續上看至 1.32 兆美元、+49%Y oY，除有
利於上修川湖 2026 年的營運預估外，2027 年營運成長仍可上看 5 成
水準 。 投顧預估2026 年營收上看 562 億元(原 440 億元)、Y oY 221%，
稅後 EPS 393.72 元，同時預估 2027 年營收將挑戰 870 億元、Y oY 
54%，稅後 EPS 612.06 元。

川湖是 亞洲第一大 伺服器導軌 製
造商

川湖是亞洲第一大伺服器導軌製造商 
川湖科技於 1986 年成立，以生產家具五金配件起家，擁有卓越機構產品設計、
模具設計與設備開發之整合設計能力並結合數十年的連續沖壓與表面處理技術
能力為根基。經過不斷地創新與研發，以自創品牌 King Slide 綠色創新機構產
品行銷全球，躍升為國際知名伺服器導軌、工業用導軌及電子零組件等機構產
品的大廠。川湖目前是亞洲第一大伺服器導軌製造商，Server 客戶過去以品牌
為主，2000 年從品牌客戶起家，2009 年起跨入高階廚具導軌市場，直至 2014
年 Server 客戶仍以品牌為主，2015 年 CSP 需求開始，川湖沒有跟上趨勢，致
2017~2020 年都很辛苦，一路追到 2022 年市佔率達 3 成左右，品牌及 CSP 的
比重正式來到 50：50，2023 年才正式突破來到 52%：48%。 另外 ，1H23 AI Server
營收比重約 8%，3Q23 約 9%，後續公司不再拆分 AI 營收比重。 
2025 年營收 175 億元 、Y oY 73%， 毛利率76.1%、Y oY 6.93pts， 營業利益120.89
億元、Y oY 98%，營益率 69.1%、Y oY 8.85pts，業外淨收入 3.35 億元，其中匯
損金額 4.4 億元， 稅前淨利124 億元 、Y oY 59%， 稅後淨利98.37 億元 、Y oY 60%，
稅後 EPS 103.23 元 。 其中 ，2025 年營收比重，導軌產品占 97.87%，其中 Server
導軌占約 88~89%，餘廚具受累美國貿易政策影響，營收比重下滑至 11~12%。

毛 利 率 顯 著 攀 升 ， 主 要 來 自
持 續 創 新 產 品 ，IP 帶來實質
獲 利 ， 大 量 縮 短 開 發 時 程 ，
亦創造高進入門檻

毛利率顯著攀升，主要來自持續創新產品，IP 帶來實質獲利，大量縮短開發時
程，亦創造高進入門檻 
川湖面對客戶持續要砍價，價格壓力仍長期存在，惟其持續創新產品，客戶用
在先進產品系列中，川湖累計至 8/6 止專利核准總數已達 3,752 個(申請 3,825
個)、+1.59%QoQ，每周 1~3 個 IP 誕生，為川湖帶來高毛利率及獲利，是最純
的 IP 股。2027~2029 年的導軌創新性更高，屆時會比 2025 年的毛利率再顯著
提升 ， 期間獲利表現可以期待 。 高毛利率並非單純是ASP 賣高所致 ， 包括設計、
模組到製造的極致化，整合能力強，川湖在前端硬體、設計架構成熟，能及時
符合客戶要求都是主要原因，而不是單純歸因於高 ASP 及自動化。毛利率正常
水準為>70%，若產品組合、匯率等因素有利，則80%以上可視為紅利挹注，並
非固定常態，而就趨勢來看，毛利率呈正向，中長期甚至可能優於目前水準。 
導軌在 Server 裡就是安全關鍵 ， 有重量 、 有精密度的產品 ， 更需求川湖的角色，
未來商機仍無限，現在沒有用導軌的客戶，未來都是川湖的機會，美國、中國
仍有很多客戶亦都沒有用，CSP 客戶加速導軌的採用趨勢，有利川湖。川湖最
大優勢在於快速整合機構設計、模具、製造及自動化。過去研發時程需要 1~2
年，現行僅約 3 個月即可完成。隨 GPU 快速迭代，致產品更迭速度加快，川湖
需快速完成開發、量產及可靠度驗證，深厚專利佈局形成進入門檻。 
近月營收顯著成長 ， 於前次法說時已預告 ， 下游ODM 客戶 Leadtime 約 2 個月 ，
因應 AI 產業算力需求增加，下游 ODM 訂單出貨持續挹注所致。

季營收達成率 47%，遠優於預期，累計營收+137%YoY ( 上月 99%)。7 月營收

資源以補充產能，目前產能不是問題，可以滿足客戶需求。1H26 川湖為多個新
專案推出了一系列尖端產品，並取得了非常穩健的上半年業績。正如過去幾年
一直強調的，川湖對突破性產品研發的投入最終將為川湖帶來繁榮。如今，這
些投入的成果正日益顯現，並持續印證著川湖向股東傳達的 訊息。展望未來，
川湖將持續加速研發 2027、2028 和 2029 年的突破性產品。這些努力將進一步
鞏固市場領先地位，並拓展產品在雲端伺服器基礎架構領域的應用。隨著人工
智慧應用的不斷加速 ，樂觀地認為 ，川湖客戶群分散幾百家 ，AI 算力需求增加，

求，2025 年 11/6 董事會通過自地委建興建廠房及員工宿舍，設立川湖二廠(有

3.4 萬坪)，預計 2027 年中完工，產能約可增 20%。

全球 CSP 上修 Capex，加上川益三、五期新廠年底動工，產能增加 2 倍有餘，
確認未來營運成長無虞，同時上修獲利及目標價 
投顧上修 2026、2027 年稅後 EPS 分別來到 393.72 元及 612.06 元，依目前股
價 11,110 元，PER 18X(2027EPS) 仍顯著落後競爭對手(高達 30X)。法說已定
調川益 3、5 期新廠建置規劃時程，將提前於今年底開始動工，亦將確認今明年
的營運成長無虞，且產能擴增 2 倍亦為未來獲利提升想像空 間，市場對川湖
2026、2027 年營運仍有再上修空間 ， 依其2027 年獲利股價仍有再上修至 18,360
元的空間(30X 2027EPS) ，故投顧維持買進投資評等，目標價暫上修至 15,300
元(25X 2027EPS)(原 11,100 元)。

2059.TT 川湖 
 能見度直達 2029

[法說會更新]   
   公司簡介 
川湖成立於 1986 年，初期生產辦公室鉸鏈與滑軌、家具五金配件產品，現為導軌製
造商，以自有品牌 King Slide 行銷產品，導軌用途涵蓋伺服器、廚具以及工業，伺服
器導軌為最大宗產品，客戶包括品牌廠(Dell、HP、IBM)與白牌客戶。    
投資評等/目標價 重點摘要 
買進 14,300 
【 前 次 投 資 建 議 】買進，5,700 
【大盤指數/股價】44226 / 11110

➢ 川湖 2Q26 毛利率飆升，EPS 創歷單季新高 
川湖 2Q26 營收為 108.3 億元，QoQ+98.7%，YoY+156.1%，毛利率
87.4%，營益率 82.1%，EPS 74.38 元，營收、獲利創單季新高，大幅優
於投研部預期。AI 伺服器需求強勁帶動川湖營收爆發性成長，公司產能
利用率由 1Q26 的 60%，提升至 2Q26 的 70%~80%；2Q26 毛利率
QoQ+9.7ppts，YoY+9.9ppts，大幅上升的原因包含 IP 帶來的效益(公
司擁有 3,800 項專利)、自動化生產使效率提升、高單價/高毛利產品出
貨增加等 。 除了上述因素 ， 影響公司毛利率的因子還有匯率(台幣升貶 1%
對毛利率影響數 0.5%~0.6%)，鋼材漲跌的對毛利率的影響相對小。  
➢ 7 月營收突破 60 億元，2H26 HoH 增幅樂觀  
川湖 7 月營收為 64.1 億元，MoM+44.2%，YoY+355.5%，單月營收再
度突破歷史新高紀錄，2H26 持續有新品量產，公司表示 2H26 HoH 幅
度值得期待。川湖在全球擁有數百家客戶，包含四大 CSP 以及新的
Hyperscalers，公司認為 2027 年~2029 年的滑軌設計創新性更高，因
此術門檻極高、難度大，川湖能夠達成由模具到量產的快速更新，對於
未來商機展望十分正向。投研部預估川湖 3Q26 營收為 165.5 億元，
QoQ+52.8%，YoY+278.7%，EPS 為 99.50 元。  
川湖看到 2027 年~2029 年 AI 伺服器、Rack 滑軌需求強勁，在台灣、
美國持續擴產 ， 最新進度有：1)美國休士頓廠為高度自動化產線 ， 預計9
月試機量產；2)川益第三期、第五期將提早動工，川益三期、五期預計
2026 年底動工(比原定提早 1~2 年)， 投資規模達百億 ， 二個廠房面積共
22 萬米平方。 
➢ 維持買進建議，目標價 14,300 元 
川湖 2Q26 營收、獲利大躍進，毛利率表現亮眼，公司表示 2H26 持續
有新品量產，營運展望樂觀，且已經與客戶著手規劃 2027 年~2029 年
之產品，川湖已在台灣、美國擴產以因應長期需求的攀升。川湖PE 區間
位於 10X~28X， 考量公司在AI 伺服器市場具主導地位 ， 握有多達3,800
項的專利，建議買進，目標價 14,300 元(2027F PE 30X)。

個股報告

川湖 2Q26 毛利率飆升，EPS 創歷單季新高 
川湖 2Q26 營收為 108.3 億元，QoQ+98.7%，YoY+156.1%，毛利率 87.4%，營益
率 82.1%，EPS 74.38 元，營收、獲利創單季新高，大幅優於投研部預期。AI 伺服器
需求強勁帶動川湖營收爆發性成長，公司產能利用率由 1Q26 的 60%，提升至 2Q26
的 70%~80%；2Q26 毛利率 QoQ+9.7ppts，YoY+9.9ppts， 大幅上升的原因包含IP
帶來的效益(公司擁有 3,800 項專利)、自動化生產使效率提升、高單價/高毛利產品出
貨增加等。除了上述因素，影響公司毛利率的因子還有匯率(台幣升貶 1%對毛利率影
響數 0.5~0.6%)，鋼材漲跌的對毛利率的影響相對小。 
圖 1：川湖產品組合 2022~2026F

資料來源：國票投顧 
7 月營收突破 60 億元，2H26 HoH 增幅樂觀 
川湖 7 月營收為 64.1 億元，MoM+44.2%，YoY+355.5%， 單月營收再度突破歷史新
高紀錄，2H26 持續有新品量產，公司表示 2H26 HoH 幅度值得期待。川湖在全球擁
有數百家客戶，包含四大 CSP 以及新的 Hyperscalers，公司認為 2027 年~2029 年
的滑軌設計創新性更高，因此術門檻極高、難度大，川湖能夠達成由模具到量產的快
速更新，對於未來商機展望十分正向。投研部預估川湖 3Q26 營收為 165.5 億元，
QoQ+52.8%，YoY+278.7%，EPS 為 99.50 元。

美國廠 9 月投產，2026 年底川益三期、五期動工 
川湖看到 2027 年~2029 年 AI 伺服器、Rack 滑軌需求強勁，本次法說宣布川益第三
期、第五期將提早動工，目前產能布局為：1)川湖一廠(路竹廠)，生產傳統鋼珠滑軌、
烤漆滑軌及隱藏式鉸鏈等家具與廚具五金；2)川湖二廠已於 2025 年底動工，包含智
慧化生產基地與員工宿舍，預計 2027 年~2028 年完成，目標再增加 20%產能；3)川
益廠一期、二期，主要用來生產伺服器導軌與相關精密滑軌 ；4)川益三期、五期預計
2026 年底動工(比原定提早 1~2 年)，投資規模達百億，二個廠房面積共 22 萬米平方
(川益 1+2 廠的 11 萬平方米的 2 倍)；5)美國休士頓廠為高度自動化產線，預計 9 月
試機量產，美國廠美國廠產品價格已經和客戶取得共識，川湖認為不需擔心利潤率問
題。 
維持買進建議，目標價 14,300 元 
川湖 2Q26 營收、獲利大躍進，毛利率表現亮眼，公司表示 2H26 持續有新品量產，
營運展望樂觀，且已經與客戶著手規劃 2027 年~2029 年之產品，已在台灣、美國擴
產以因應長期需求的攀升。川湖 PE 區間位於 10X~28X，考量公司在 AI 伺服器市場
具主導地位 ， 握有多達3,800 項的專利 ，建議買進 ， 目標價14,300 元(2027F PE 30X)。

公司簡介 
高階機構件滑軌領導品牌製造商 ， 產品應用
在伺服器及廚具 。 隨著近年伺服器市場蓬勃
發展 ， 伺服器導軌已經成為川湖的最核心產
品應用 ， 營收比重高達約9 成 。 川湖具備高
客製化研發能力，以及 3,752 項的專利護
城河 ， 有利為客戶整合開發設計最新且快速
迭代的伺服器規格，推算 AI 伺服器供應市
占率達 80%~90%以上。

主要客戶：伺服器品牌商及美系大型 CSP 
主要競爭對手：

黃璽銘 hsiming@sinopac.com 
本次報告：法說會

川湖 (2059 TT) 
大幅優於預期

永豐觀點 
導軌設計複雜度提高，領先設計開發，高市佔率供應，價值體現於營收獲利成長。 
 投資評價與建議 
研究部維持川湖買進建議：川湖 2Q26 財報獲利及 7 月營收大幅優於預期。川湖
維持 AI 伺服器導軌領導地位，隨著客戶不斷升級AI 伺服器機櫃硬體架構產品，設
計複雜度提高 ， 導軌產品價值提升幅度優於預估 ， 且高市佔率受惠資本支出投入AI
伺服器擴張的趨勢，強勁動能延續至 2027 年後。公司新產能的開出，自動化比例
繼續提高，在拓展營收規模的同時，將能維持獲利率水準。預估2026 年/2027 年
稅後 EPS 327.67元/445.79 元 。 維持買進建議 ， 上修目標價至13,375 元(30 x2027F 
EPS)。

ESG 評析 
川湖企業永續評鑑整體為 B+等，於 SASB 產業永續議題與跨產業五大永續面向平
均得分分別為 A 等與 B+等。 
SinoPac+ ESG 評鑑系統評等

資料來源：SinoPac+ ESG 評等 (依循 2024 SinoPac+企業永續評鑑方法學) 
註 1：資誠永續發展服務股份有限公司僅於ＥＳＧ評鑑系統方法學建置過程中，就評分指標提供
專業意見，對於評分結果及評估報告內容之完整性及真實性，不負擔保責任，亦不對閱讀或使用

高階機構件滑軌領導品牌製造商：川湖產品應用在伺服器及廚具。 隨著近年伺服器
市場蓬勃發展 ， 伺服器導軌已經成為川湖的最核心產品應用 ， 營收比重高達約9 成。
川湖具備高客製化研發能力 ， 以及3,752 項的專利護城河 ， 有利為客戶整合開發設
計最新且快速迭代的伺服器規格 。川湖與各大伺服器客戶合作 ，囊括伺服器品牌商
及美系大型 CSP，推算 AI 伺服器供應市占率達 80%~90%以上。 
2023 年以前川湖伺服器產品中，以通用伺服器導軌為主，隨著 AI 伺服器需求爆
發 ， 對伺服器機櫃的承重及整體效能要求大幅提升， 帶動高階滑軌規格升級及強勁
需求 。 同時帶動川湖毛利率上升至7 成以上 ， 成為台灣 AI 伺服器供應鏈高獲利的
利基型大廠。依產品組合區分，2Q26 年營收比重：導軌 99.3%、滑軌 0.02%、鉸
鏈 0.2%、其他 0.5%。 
2Q26 財報大幅優於預期：川湖 2Q26 稅後 EPS 74.38 元，毛利率87.4%、營益率
82.1%，皆大幅優於預期，受惠 AI 伺服器輝達 GB300、Blackwell HGX 平台及
AWS Trainium 3 量產規模放大，新平台提高導軌產品的價值較預期高，公司自動
化生產效益顯著提升，毛利率及營益率進一步躍升至8 成以上。2Q26 未分配盈餘
稅約 2.5 億，稅後淨利 70.9 億元，季增 103%，年增 1,054%。 
2H26 營收動能更強勁：7 月營收 64 億元，月增 44%，年增 356%，營收達成率
66%，同樣大幅優於預期。推算包含GB300、B300 HGX、Trainium3、TPU 等 AI
伺服器導軌產品價值較原預期高，且客戶增加訂單，產品放量後帶動營收強勁成
長。預期9 月開始出貨 Vera Rubin 導軌產品，將延續營收動能。上修預估3Q26、
4Q26 營收至 161 億、162 億，毛利率及營益率分別維持在約 85%、80%，稅後
EPS 107.41 元、109.30 元。 
維持 AI 伺服器導軌領導地位 產品價值顯現：先前雖然 AI 伺服器導軌市場同業競
爭市占傳聞不斷，但以川湖財報成績推算，川湖目前在 GB、HGX 系列 AI 伺服器
與 ASIC AI 伺服器導軌市場供應佔有高達 80%以上甚至 90%以上的市佔率，其最
大的護城河來自於強大的客製化研發能力與深厚的專利佈局 。 隨著客戶不斷升級AI
伺服器機櫃硬體架構，機櫃導入液冷散熱設計、產品重量提高，川湖配合客戶開發
導軌需要的新作動功能、增加機構件、提升負荷強度、配合有限空間安裝，整體設
計複雜度提升，公司的客製化設計能力反應在產品價值上揚。且公司成本、費用控
制得宜，營收規模放大的同時，進一步帶動獲利率擴張。 
高市佔率受惠資本支出投入 AI 伺服器上升趨勢：美系 CSP、Neo Cloud 持續增加
資本支出(含四大 CSP、Oracle、Coreweave、SpaceX)，目前 Bloomberg 預估
2026 年、2027 年支出將年增 98%、41%，川湖的高市佔率及跟隨客戶優先開發
新品的技術能力，將優先受惠無論是 GPU、ASIC AI 伺服器的建置需求。另外，近
期 CPU 運算推升通用伺服器需求，以及在輝達 Vera Rubin 世代開始量產後，非
運算櫃的電源、儲存、網通、推論晶片等機櫃形式需求將開始增加，預期帶動導軌
另一大成長動能。 
強勁動能延續至 2027 年：2Q26 財報、7 月營收公告大幅優於預期，推算川湖導
軌產品價量提升，將持續於 ASIC、VR200、MI450 等新一代 AI 伺服器機櫃顯現，
下半年營收動能持續看增至 2027 年，且新產能的開出，自動化比例繼續提高，在
拓展營收規模的同時，將能維持獲利率水準。上調2026 年營收至 485 億元，年增
177%，毛利率 84.9%，營益率 78.9%，稅後淨利 312 億元，年增 217%，稅後
EPS 327.67 元。預期 2027 年 VR200、MI450、Trainium 3氣冷水冷、TPUv8 等
AI 伺服器機櫃及非運算櫃的需求續增、以及 CPU 通用伺服器需求成長放大。整體
市場增加川湖導軌需求的產值優於預期，上修預估 2027 年營收 656 億元，年增
35%，毛利率 85.2%，營益率 80.1%，稅後淨利 425 億元，稅後 EPS 445.79 元。

1 群益投顧 
台積電 (2330 TT) 公告 4,675.81 億元， MoM+5.62% ， YoY+44.68% 。 台積電競爭力優
於同業， 建議 台 積 電 Buy ，目標價 3,200 元 。

研 究 員 ： 陳 俐 妍   
ly.c@capital.com.tw

07/202 6 營收 4, 675.81 億元， MoM +5 .62 %， YoY+ 44 .68 %：  
台積電 公告 07/2026 營收 4,675.81 億 元 ，MoM+5.62% ， YoY+44.68% 。 先前
台 積 電 法 說 會預估 3Q2 6 營收 446 ~458 億 美 元 ，QoQ+ 10 .94 %~+ 13 .94 %，中
間值 +12 .44 %。在 1 美元 兌 新 台 幣 32.0 元 基礎上 ， 新 台 幣 營 收 將 為 1. 43 兆
元 ~1. 47 兆 元 ，QoQ+ 13 .86 %~+ 15.37 %。 以 此 推 估 ，08/2026 和 09/2026 單月
平 均 營 收 在 4, 79 8 億元 ~4, 990 億元， MoM+ 2.62 %~+ 7.72 %。  
消費者和對價格敏感的終端市場領域，受到零組件價格上漲以及宏觀經濟
不 確 定 性 的 影 響，正 承 受 著 挑 戰。因 此 台 積 電 在 業 務 規 劃 上 保 持 審 慎，儘 管
如 此 ， 與  AI 相 關 的 需 求 依 然 極 其 強 勁 。 受 到 先 進 製 程 技 術 的 強 勁 需 求 支
持，其中 N2 技 術 的 快 速 產 能 爬 坡 。 
2Q26 毛 利 率 方 面 ， 台 積 電 預 估 3Q26 毛 利 率 將 介 於 65% - 67% 之 間 ， 以
中間值  66% 計 算 ， 較  2Q26 的 實 際 毛 利 率  67.72% 下 降 約  1.7 個百分
點 。 這 主 要 是 因 為 N2 技 術 的 快 速 爬 坡 ， 預 期 將 稀 釋 毛 利 率 約 3 - 4 個 百 分
點，此 稀 釋 將 被 領 先 技 術 的 強 勁 需 求 與 持 續 的 成 本 改 善（ 含 生 產 力 提 升 ）部
分 抵 消。匯 率 假 設 方 面，3Q26 預 估 以 美 元 兌 新 台 幣 32.00 元 為 假 設，預 估
台積電 3Q26 稅後 EPS 27.51 元。  
台 積 電 競 爭 力 優 於 同 業 ，建議 Buy ：  
台 積電 將 有 望 再 提 高 2024~2029 年  AI 加 速 器 和 CPU 等 營 收 之 年 複 合 成
長率  (CAGR) 55% - 59% 。 即 使 消 費 性 電 子 （ 如 PC 、 智 慧 型 手 機 ） 等 價 格
敏 感 市 場 受 到 零 件 漲 價 與 總 經 不 確 定 性 影 響，但 AI 需 求 極 度 強 勁。由 於 AI
正 從 生 成 式（ 詢 問 模 式 ）轉 向 代 理 式/專 注 型（ 指 令 與 行 動 模 式 ） ，大 幅 增 加
Token 的 消 耗 量 與 運 算 需 求 ，並增加 CPU 的 需 求 。台 積 電 將 2026 年 美 元 營
收 成 長 率 預 期 上 修 ，YoY>+ 40% 。 預 估 台 積 電 2026 年 稅 後 EPS 106 .46 元。
隨 CSP 大 廠 進 行 大 規 模 布 局 ， 有 利 台 積 電 訂 單 增 加， 且 競 爭 力 優 於 同 業 ，
投資評等 Buy ， 目 標 價 3,200 元。

2 群益投顧 
  【 投 資 評 等 說 明 】 
  評等  定義  
  強力買進 (Strong Buy)  首 次 評 等 潛 在 上 漲 空 間≥ 35%  
  買進 (Buy)  15%≤ 首 次 評 等 潛 在 上 漲 空 間<35%  
  區間操作 (Trading Buy)  5%≤ 首 次 評 等 潛 在 上 漲 空 間<15%

中立 (Neutral)  
無 法 由 基 本 面 給 予 投 資 評 等 
預 期 近 期 股 價 將 處 於 盤 整 
建 議 降 低 持 股

【 投 資 評 等 調 整 機 制 】 
  投 資 評 等 係「 首 次 」給 予 特 定 個 股 投 資 評 等 時，其 前 一 日 收 盤 價 相 較3 個月
目 標 價 之 潛 在 上 漲 空 間 計 算 而 得 。 個 股 投 資 評 等 分 為四 個 等 級 ， 定 義 如 上 。
爾 後 的 投 資 評 等係 依 循 「 首 次 評 等 」 ， 直 到 停 止 推 薦 。  
「 強 力 買 進 」、「 買 進 」及 「 區 間 操 作 」均有 upside 目 標 價 。差 別 僅 在 於 ，
首 次 評 等 時 潛 在 報 酬 率 不 同 。 「 中 立 」 則 無 目 標 價 。 
一 旦 我 們 給 予 特 定 個 股「 強 力 買 進 」、「 買 進 」或「 區 間 操 作 」之 投 資 評 等，
就 是 責 任 的 開 始，爾 後 將 透 過 各 式 研 究 報 告 作 定 期 性、持 續 性 追 蹤 基 本 面 及
股 價 變 化 ， 直 到 停 止 推 薦 。 
停 止 推 薦 情 境 ： 
1.  達 目 標 價 。 
2.  雖 未 達 原 訂 目 標 價 ， 但 檢 視 基 本 面 、 訊 息 面 、 籌 碼 面 等 多 方 訊 息 ， 研
判 股 價 上 漲 空 間 已 然 有 限 ， 將 適 時 出 具 降 評 報 告 。 
3.  推 薦 後 股 價 不 漲 反 跌 ， 亦 將 出 具 降 評 報 告 。

【免責聲明】  
  本 研 究 報 告 僅 提 供 予 特 定 人 之 客 戶 作 為 參 考 資 料「 非 經 同 意 不 得 轉 載 」 。我
們 並 不 確 保 此 資 訊 的 完 整 性 與 正 確 性，投 資 人 應 了 解，報 告 中 有 關 未 來 預 測
之 陳 述 可 能 不 會 實 現，因 而 不 應 被 依 賴。而 且 此 報 告 並 非 根 據 特 定 投 資 目 的
或 依 預 定 對 象 之 財 務 狀 況 所 撰 寫 出 來 的，因 此，此 研 究 報 告 的 目 的，既 非 對
投 資 人 於 買 賣 證 券、選 擇 權、期 貨 或 其 他 證 券 相 關 之 衍 生 性 商 品 提 供 詢 價 服
務，亦 非 作 為 進 行 交 易 的 要 約。投 資 人 應 注 意 到 相 關 證 券 之 價 值 及 收 益，可
能會有無預警地上升或下降，產生投資回報金額可能比原始投資來得少的
情形。

華 邦 電 為 利 基 型 記 憶 體 製 造
廠，可根據產 業市況彈性 調整
DRAM 和 Flash 產能分配

2Q26 EPS 5.4 元優於預 期，
3Q26 記憶體漲價趨勢將延續

華邦電合併營收包含記憶體業務以及新唐， 就記憶體業務營收結構而言 ，Flash
和 CMS(Specialty DRAM) 業務分別占 2Q26 營收比重為 61%以及 39%。以產
品應用而言，通訊電子、電腦、消費性電子、車用及工業用分別占 2Q26 記憶
體業務 29%、15%、28%、28%，其中以通訊營收+74%QoQ 表現最佳。主要
客戶包含手機廠商、歐美日汽車廠以及 NB 廠商。華邦電 2026 年資本支出為
395 億元遠高於 2025 年之 55 億元 ，生產設備約占 95%， 高雄廠年底月產能提
升 24K，至於台中廠月產能則為 51-52K，其中 DRAM 產能由 12K 降至 7K，
公司可根據產業市況彈性調整 DRAM 和 Flash 產能分配。至於市場期待的
CUBE 以及矽電容業務，公司定調此兩業務將帶動 2027 年後新一輪成長。

華邦電記憶體業務產品組合和毛利率

Source：Company

2Q26 營收 598.4 億元(+56%QoQ)，成長主要來自於記憶體業務，其中 CMS
業務 ASP 漲幅高於 Flash 業務，毛利率 66.2%優於於前季 53.4%，營業利益
289.7 億元(+131%QoQ)，業外認列收益 10.39 億元，稅後純益 243.1 億元
(+140%QoQ)，EPS 5.4 元優於預期之 3.67 元。展望 3Q26，不論 DRAM 或是
Flash 漲價循環皆延續，估計 3Q26 營收 766.1 億元(QoQ+28%)，受惠於記憶
體業務出貨 ASP 持續上揚，毛利率提升至 71.7%，EPS 7.52 元。

個股報告

不論 CMS 或是 Flash 業務供不
應，及價格上漲趨勢尚未看到終
點

高雄廠將啟動 Module B 投資，
2029 年產能開出

維持買進，目標價 225 元

在 CMS 業務方面，2Q26 CMS 營收+78%QoQ，成長主要來自價格上漲。AI 
GPU/CPU/ASIC 驅動的 HBM、DDR5 和 LP DDR5 需求大幅成長，AI 基礎建
設則帶動 eSSD、SmartNIC 與 BMC 採用更高規格 DRAM 因應高速資料處理、
傳輸和系統管理要求 ， 至於非AI 應用產能受限 ， 原廠對客戶擁有更佳之產品訂
價能力。在產業供給方面，雖供應商整體擴充產能，但產能規劃將搭配客戶長
約需求，並減少 DDR4 和 LP DDR4 等成熟製程產能產出，產業供不應求將延
續數季，集邦預估 3Q26 傳統型 DRAM 價格季增 13-18%。2027 年華邦出貨
量成長主要來自於高雄廠產能提升以及 16nm 製程轉換效益，至於應用需求則
以 eSSD 最為看好，可望占華邦 CMS 業務 10%營收。

在 Flash 業務上，2Q26 Flash 營收+65%QoQ，量價皆較上季成長。最新世代
AI 機櫃 NOR Flash 用量高達 500-600 顆，華邦市占高達 40-50%，AI 應用為
High Density 出貨成長最佳領域，至於 SLC NAND 除受惠於國際大廠退出 2D 
NAND 市場 ， 以及eMMC 供不應求致使客戶回頭採購 SLC Flash， 價格漲勢以
及毛利率皆優於 NOR Flash。華邦 2027 年 Flash 產能將由 40K 提升至 50K，
位元成長上看 40%+並優於今年之 20%+，其中 SLC NAND 出貨成長性將優於
NOR Flash，公司冀望挑戰 SLC NAND 市占龍頭目標。

SLC NAND Flash 合約價格漲勢預估

Source：Trendforce

由於目前 DRAM 供給極為吃緊，即便明年高雄廠產能提升至 24K 仍無法滿足
客戶需求，已有大量客戶想與公司簽訂 LTA，故高雄廠將啟動 Module B 擴建
計畫，2027 年 1 月動工，目標於 2029 年初導入設備，2029 年中開始試產，
2029 年底量產，並投入 12nm、14nm 和 16nm，依照無塵室空間，整個投資
計畫至多可新增 50-60K 投片產能，但第一階段僅先安裝 10-20K 產能，EUV
需待第二階段擴產才會導入，Module B 產能已有客戶洽談 LTA。此外，公司
表示目前出貨以季合約價為主，並未皆露 LTA 客戶營收占比。

2026 年和 2027 年 EPS 分別上修至 24.43 元和 47.63 元。考量今明年獲利持
續上修 ， 並啟動高雄廠擴產計畫 ， 維持買進評等 ，TP 225 元( 2027 PBR x 2.6)。

評等分級：買進（預期未來六個月股價漲幅超過+30%）; 逢低買進（預期未來六個月股價漲幅介於+15%與+30%之間）; 區間操
作（預期未來六個月股價漲跌幅介於-15%與+15%之間）; 逢高賣出（預期未來六個月股價跌幅介於-15%與-30%之間）；賣出（預
期未來六個月股價跌幅超過-30%）。

- 1 - 
個股報告 
義隆(2458) 
NB缺料導致 26H2受影響 
投資評等 目標價 
區間操作 $150 
前次投資建議 2026.3.5： 
「區間操作」 ，目標價：$175

報告日期：2026.8.10 研 究 員：林秀泓

摘 要 及 調 整 原 因 
➢ CPU、記憶體缺貨比預期嚴重，導致 26H2 營收受到影響。 
➢ 無人機產品開始出貨，成為新的營收動能。

基本資料 結論與建議 
2026/8/7 收盤價： 141.50 ➢ 26Q2 營收 32.1 億元，QoQ-0.6%，YoY+5.7%，靠近財
測 QoQ-0.8%~+5.4%下緣。毛利率 47.4%(QoQ-0.2pt，
YoY-0.7pt)，下降主要因為晶圓代工成本上升影響。EPS 
2.89 元。 
➢ TouchPad方面，haptic產品已開始出貨，ASP較傳統產
品高出 5~6 倍。義隆和微軟合作開發 advance 功能，隨
不同 APP 產生不同的振動回饋，是目前市場上唯一量產
的合作夥伴，Dell、HP 正在規劃跟進。 
➢ AI 產品線以無人機應用為核心，AI BOX 於 2026/7 開始
出貨，單價 300~500 美元，遠優於傳統鏡頭模組 40~50
美元或 ISP 5~6 美元。和雷虎合作的產品已通過測試並出
貨。2026 年底前推出避開障礙功能。 
➢ 展望 26H2，因為供應商產能轉往 AI server，CPU、記憶
體缺貨比預期嚴重，導致 26H2 營收受到影響。調整預估
2026/2027 年 營 收123.4 億元 /130.2 億元，
YoY+0.1%/+5.5% ，淨利 27.1 億元 /27.8 億元，
YoY+10.9%/+2.6%，EPS 8.92 元/9.15元。 
➢ 目前股價處於 16X 26(F) PER / 15X 27(F) PER，為公司
近五年 PE 8~23X 區間中位。2026 年度配息預估 7.7 元
(2027 年配發)，推估殖利率5.4%。考量下有殖利率支撐，
但營運動能減緩，投資評等維持「區間操作」 ，下調6 個
月目標價 150 元(17X 26(F) PER  / 16X 27(F) PER)，潛
在上漲空間 6%。

資料來源：CMoney；兆豐國際預估

營 運 分 析

義隆為全球前五大 IC設計公司 
義隆成立於 1994/05，於 2001/09 上市掛牌，為台灣IC 設計公司，擁有兩大
產品類別與五大產品線，觸控類產品有觸控螢幕晶片、觸控板模組、指紋辨
識晶片等，非觸控類產品則包括指向裝置、MCU 等，26Q2 營收占比分別為
TouchPad 38%、TouchScreen 21%、指紋辨識 11%、指向裝置 15%、MCU 
3%、AI 12%。在筆電觸控領域，義隆的產品具有指標地位，觸控板、螢幕
觸控 IC、指向裝置全球市佔第一，指紋辨識晶片全球市佔第二。

圖一、義隆 26Q2 產品應用比重

資料來源：義隆；兆豐國際彙整

26Q2營收符合財測，靠近下緣 
26Q2 營收 32.1億元，QoQ-0.6%，YoY+5.7%，靠近財測 QoQ-0.8%~+5.4%
的下緣。毛利率 47.4%(QoQ-0.2pt，YoY-0.7pt)，毛利率下降主要因為晶圓
代工成本上升影響。營業利益率下降到 21.7%(QoQ-2.4pt，YoY-2.8pt)。業
外方面，主要來自金融資產評價損益佔 85%，其餘15 來自利息收入和其他，

26Q3 未提供財測  
根據規定，近 4 季業外收支佔稅前損益>10%，不提供具體財測。以營收比
重而言，AI 營收佔比上升，Touch 比例和 26Q2 持平，Non-touch 比重下滑
1~2%。毛利率因為晶圓廠 26Q3 繼續漲價，加上材料成本上升，預期26Q3
毛利率下降。因應成本增加，包括記憶體、CPU、被動元件漲價，以及晶圓
代工成本上升，公司於 2026/7/1 起調漲產品價格，漲幅 5~10%。 
參考 2026/07 月營收 我們估計 26Q3 營收 8.8 億元， MoM-17.1%，
YoY-13.2%，我們預期 26Q3 營收季減。預估 26Q3 營收 29.6 億元，
QoQ-7.7%，YoY-10.7%，毛利率47.2%，營業利益5.91 億元，QoQ-15.2%，

前市場上唯一量產的合作夥伴，Dell、HP正在規劃跟進。FingerPrint 方面，

- 5 - 
個股報告 
2026/7 開始出貨，單價 300~500 美元，遠優於傳統鏡頭模組 40~50 美元或
ISP 5~6 美元。和雷虎合作的產品已通過測試並出貨。2026 年底前推出避開
障礙功能，4 鏡頭方案約 500 美元，多鏡頭方案 1000 美元以上。

缺料導致營運動能減緩 
展望 26H2，因為供應商產能轉往 AI server，CPU、記憶體缺貨比預期嚴重，
導致 26H2 營收受到影響。調整預估 2026/2027年營收 123.4億元/130.2 億
元，YoY+0.1%/+5.5%，淨利 27.1 億元/27.8億元，YoY+10.9%/+2.6%，EPS 
8.92 元/9.15 元。

圖四、2026 有望重回成長 
義隆各年度營收及獲利走勢預估(百萬元)

資料來源：義隆；兆豐國際預估

評價中位，下有殖利率支撐 
義隆目前股價處於 16X 26(F) PER / 15X 27(F) PER，為公司近五年 PE 
8~23X 區間中位。殖利率方面，義隆指出未來配發率 8 成以上，2026 年度
配息預估 7.7 元(2027年配發)，推估殖利率 5.4%。考量下有殖利率支撐，但
營運動能減緩，投資評等維持「區間操作」 ，下調 6 個月目標價 150 元(17X 
26(F) PER / 16X 27(F) PER)，潛在上漲空間 6%。

研 究  報 告                
義隆(2458.TT) 
產業：電子–半導體 
2H26 僅賴無人機支撐營運，動能受限 
 投資評價與建議 
2H26 NB客戶拉貨力道轉弱將壓抑整體營運動能，公司已通過1H26配發4.4
元，預估2026年配發7.4元，殖利率約4.6%，具下檔保護及優良⻑投標的。  
 重點評論分析 
3Q26 NB拉貨趨緩，營運將持續季減：客戶面臨缺料問題，拉貨將趨緩，營運
將較1H26辛苦，預估營收29.1億元，QoQ-9.3%，雖7/1全面漲價，漲幅約
5~10%，然仍持續面臨供應鏈成本壓力，預估毛利率47%，EPS 1.88元。 
僅賴非NB產品支撐營運 ， 2H26動能有限： 展望2H26 ， NB客戶面臨更大的CPU
與記憶體缺料問題，而義隆營收約90%以上與NB相關，因此營運也將受影響，
主要動能來自非NB產品的無人機：公司與中研院合作，利用自身演算法優勢，
推出Mini LED local dimming(區域調光)與ISP產品，1H26出貨以ISP為主，
ASP約5~6美元，7月已開始出貨AI Box，ASP約300~500美元，已通過雷虎測
試並開始出貨，2H26 ASP將明顯提升，將成為2026年最成⻑動能，更有望推
升2026年AI產品線挑戰雙位數營收占比 。 整體而言 ， 2H26營運僅仰賴非NB產
品支撐，然NB拉貨力道趨緩仍將壓抑整體營運動能，展望2027年，隨著缺料
問題逐步改善，NB有望逐步回復拉貨動能，搭配基期較低的無人機產品持續
成⻑，營運成⻑性將優於2026年。 
 投資風險 
NB需求進⼀步下滑或非NB貢獻不如預期。

### Page 2

2

| 營運概況  
2Q26費用較高導致本業獲利低於預期，因業外金融評價利益使得獲利優於預期：NB拉貨力道趨緩，僅AI受惠無
人機產品出貨成⻑，2Q26營收32億元，QoQ-0.6%，因供應鏈成本提升，毛利率47.4%，費用因提列較高員工酬
勞而提升至8.2億元，高於往常約7~8億元，業外約85%來自金融評價利益(多數來自投資旭暉(6698))，EPS 3.07
元，優於預期。 
3Q26 NB拉貨趨緩，營運將持續季減：客戶面臨CPU、記憶體缺料問題，拉貨將趨緩，營運將較1H26辛苦，且先
前連四季業外金額變動較大，故不提供財測，預估營收29.1億元，QoQ-9.3%，雖7/1全面漲價，漲幅約5~10%，
然仍持續面臨供應鏈成本壓力，預估毛利率47%，EPS 1.88元。 
NB面臨缺料問題，僅賴非NB產品支撐營運，2H26整體動能有限：展望2H26，NB客戶面臨更大的CPU與記憶體
缺料問題，而義隆營收約90%以上與NB相關，因此營運也將受影響，公司看法也由前次法說的不悲觀轉為營運較
1H26辛苦，主要動能來自非NB產品，1)彩色電子書：為電子書產業注入新希望，公司目前已打入第二與第三大電
子書客戶，也積極爭取第⼀大客戶，2026年出貨有望年增；2)無人機：公司與中研院合作，利用自身演算法優勢，
推出Mini LED local dimming(區域調光)與ISP產品， 1H26出貨以ISP為主， ASP約5~6美元， 7月已開始出貨AI Box ，
ASP約300~500美元，已通過雷虎測試並開始出貨，目前僅作前端光學模組，2026年底將推出避障功能，四鏡頭
方案約500美元，十二鏡頭方案將達1,000美元，2H26 ASP將明顯提升，將成為2026年最成⻑動能，更有望推升
2026年AI產品線挑戰雙位數營收占比，且公司具備MIT優勢，將積極爭取國防訂單，未來具成⻑潛力。整體而言，
2H26營運僅仰賴非NB產品支撐，然NB拉貨力道趨緩仍將壓抑整體營運動能，展望2027年，隨著缺料問題逐步改
善，NB有望逐步回復拉貨動能，搭配基期較低的無人機產品持續成⻑，營運成⻑性將優於2026年。 
2H26營運動能受限，維持中立評等，惟1H25配發現金股利4.4元，為良好⻑投標的：2H26 NB客戶拉貨力道轉弱
將壓抑整體營運動能，公司已通過1H26配發4.4元，預估2026年配發7.4元，殖利率約4.6%，具下檔保護及優良⻑
投標的。

營收走勢圖

研究員/吳大任 TERRY.WU@uni-psg.com

研究部對義隆電的投資建議調降至中立，係因(1)2Q26 本業獲利受
到成本侵蝕，不如預期，(2)受到 CPU 缺貨影響，對3Q26 營收及獲
利有所下修，(3)下修下半年的營運動能至低於上半年 ，(4)下修 26
年獲利預估，(5)期待無人機貢獻有更進一步的提升 ，綜合以上，
下修義隆電 26 年 EPS 預估為 9.36 元(原估 9.83 元，+16.5%YOY)，
及預估 27 年 EPS 為 10.14 元，+8.3%YOY)。 研究部先前給予的目標
價 158 元到價後，因為 NB 市場除了先前的記憶體影響外，目前更
受到 CPU 缺貨的影響，短線上義隆電的營運動能受到連帶影響大，
故將投資建議調降至中立，目標價 172 元。

重點摘要： 
1. 2Q26 各項財務數字皆落在財測下緣：  
義隆電2Q26 營收為落在財測區間下緣的 32.05 億元(財測
為 32-34 億元)，衰退0.6%QOQ，成長5.7%YOY，營收下滑
研究員推估為客戶提前備貨逐步告一段落所致。毛利率下
滑至 47.4%(財測區間為 46.5%-49.5%之間)，主要還是受
到各元件及晶圓代工與封測漲價，遞延反映給客戶導致毛
利率下滑 。 而OPM 為 21.76%(財測為 21.4-25.9%)之間 ， 整
體本業不如預期。而業外因為持有 6698 旭輝應材的評價
利益，加上轉投資益鼎投資的利益大幅貢獻約 2.5 億元以
上，使稅後淨利為大幅優於市場預期的 8.79 億元，成長
24.7%QOQ， 成長149%YOY，EPS 2.89 元。2Q26 產品組合為 ：
AI 12% ，觸控 70%，非觸控 18%，其中 TOUCH PAD 38%，
TOUCH SCREEN 21% ， 指 紋 11% ，POINT STICK 1 5% ，
MCU+SENSOR 3%。

2. 3Q26 未給展望，但受到 CPU 缺貨影響嚴重： 
義隆電因為最近四季合併（個別）財務報表營業外收支淨
額合計數占稅前損益合計數之絕對值大於 10%，在法說會
中不能提及預測性資訊，但在法說會有提到 CPU 缺貨狀況
比預期嚴重，加上記憶體持續高漲影響消費者購買需求，
另外客戶提前備貨也告一段落，故預告下半年動能將低於
上半年。故下修義隆電3Q26 營收預估為 30.85 億元(原估
35.3 億元)，衰退3.7%QOQ，衰退6.9%YOY，稅後淨利同步
下修預估為 6.23 億元(原估 7.84 億元)，衰退29.1%QOQ，
衰退 22.5%YOY，EPS 2.05 元(原估 2.58 元)。

### Page 2

請參見末頁重要批露及免責聲明  2

3. 期待無人機貢獻性加大： 
1H26 營收占比約低個位數 ， 主 要 出 Mini LED local 
dimming 及 ISP 晶片，單價約在個位數至十位數美金，。
不過下半年開始出相關的 AI BOX，目前已經出貨至國內無
人機廠商，整合鏡頭模組及視覺導航等功能，單價從數十
美金到數百美金不等，整體營收有望達到中高個位數。展
望 27 年，在高單價的 AI BOX 及整合相關模組開始順利出
貨下，推估營收占比有望達到雙位數。

4. 下修26年預估，27年將緩步成長： 
2Q26受到客戶已經提前拉貨影響，加上相關零組件及封裝
成本提升，使得2Q26義隆電本業獲利不如預期，所幸業外
獲利大幅挹注，使得2Q26 EPS大幅優於預期。然而，3Q26 
PC相關供應鏈受到CPU缺貨及DRAM漲價壓抑買氣，故有所
下修 。 綜合以上 ， 下修26年營收預估為126.8億元(原估137
億元)， 成長2.9%YOY， 稅後淨利同步下修預估為28.46億元
(原估29.87億元)，成長16.5%YOY，EPS 9.36元(原估9.83
元)。 展望27年 ， 目前研究部以27年NB整體出貨量低個位數
增長，加上義隆電無人機相關產品推進至整合型產品，單
價大幅提升下，預估27年營收為140億元，成長10.4%YOY，
稅後淨利預估為30.8億元，成長8.3%YOY，EPS 10.14元。

Bull/Bear: NYPCB (8046.TW)

### Page 6

NYPCB (8046.TW)
06 August 2026 Citi Research
6
NYPCB
Company description
Established in 1997, Nan Ya Printed Circuit Board (NYPCB) is a technology
subsidiary of the Formosa Plastics Group. Nan Ya PCB's main products
include conventional PCB, HDI, Flip Chip substrate (FCBGA and FCCSP), Wire
Bond Substrate, etc. The company has manufacturing sites in Taiwan and
Kunshan, China.
Investment strategy
We rate NYPCB shares as Buy. We believe ABF/BT should see a solid recovery

in 2026 on robust AI demand: 1) NYPCB is the purest ABF play with a
significant earnings turnaround, driven by growing chip sizes and layer counts
for AI ASIC/networking; and 2) the BT business should benefit from price
hikes on rising memory demand and T glass shortage. Considering T glass
support from NYPCB’s parent company, we believe NYPCB would  have a
better chance to win additional AI ASIC/networking business going forward.
Valuation
Our DCF-based TP for NYPCB is NT$1,600. With a risk-free rate of 4.30% (10-

year government bond rate), a market risk premium of 7% and an equity beta
of 1.22 (YTD weekly share price), we calculate NYPCB's WACC as 12.8%. Our
TP is equivalent to 94x/30x 2026E EPS/BVPS, which reflects its high P/E
multiple during upcycles. NYPCB traded at 15-20x P/E during its 2003-2008
upcycle driven by PC/NB demand strength. In 2010-11 when the market was
recovering from the global financial crisis, NYPCB traded at 25-35x P/E. In the
last 2019-22 upcycle when ABF was in shortage, NYPCB traded at 30-40x

P/E.
Risks
Key downside risks that could prevent the NYPCB shares from reaching our
target price include: 1) weaker-than-expected ABF demand from key
customers; 2) BT business seasonality weakness and fewer-than-expected
price hikes; and 3) severe T glass shortages that cause a bottleneck to its
production.

Guide to Citi Research Fundamental Research Investment Ratings:
Citi Research stock recommendations include an investment rating and an optional risk rating to highlight high risk stocks.

Risk rating takes into account both price volatility and fundamental criteria. Stocks will either have no risk rating or a High risk
rating assigned.
Investment Ratings: Citi Research investment ratings are Buy, Neutral and Sell. Our ratings are a function of analyst
expectations of expected total return ("ETR") and risk. ETR is the sum of the forecast price appreciation (or depreciation) plus
the dividend yield for a stock within the next 12 months.  The target price is based on a 12 month time horizon. The Investment

rating definitions are: Buy (1) ETR of 15% or more or 25% or more for High risk stocks; and Sell (3) for negative ETR. Any covered
stock not assigned a Buy or a Sell is a Neutral (2). For stocks rated Neutral (2), if an analyst believes that there are insufficient
valuation drivers and/or investment catalysts to derive a positive or negative investment view, they may elect with the approval
of Citi Research management not to assign a target price and, thus, not derive an ETR. Citi Research may suspend its rating

and target price and assign “Rating Suspended” status for regulatory and/or internal policy reasons. Citi Research may also
suspend its rating and target price and assign "Under Review" status for other exceptional circumstances (e.g. lack of
information critical to the analyst's thesis, trading suspension) affecting the company and/or trading in the company's
securities. In both such situations, the rating and target price will show as “—“ and “-“ respectively in the rating history price

chart. Prior to 11 April 2022 Citi Research assigned “Under Review” status to both situations and prior to 11 Nov 2020 only in
exceptional circumstances. As soon as practically possible, the analyst will publish a note re-establishing a rating and
investment thesis. Investment ratings are determined by the ranges described above at the time of initiation of coverage, a
change in investment and/or risk rating, or a change in target price (subject to limited management discretion). At times, the

expected total returns may fall outside of these ranges because of market price movements and/or other short-term volatility
or trading patterns. Such interim deviations will be permitted but will become subject to review by Research Management.
Your decision to buy or sell a security should be based upon your personal investment objectives and should be made only
after evaluating the stock's expected performance and risk.
Catalyst Watch/Short Term Views (“STV”) Ratings Disclosure:

Catalyst Watch and STV Upside/Downside calls: Citi Research may also include a Catalyst Watch or STV Upside or Downside
call to indicate the analyst expects the share price to rise (fall) in absolute terms over a specified period of 30 or 90 days in
reaction to one or more specific near-term catalysts or events impacting the company or the market. A Catalyst Watch will be
published when Analyst confidence is high that an impact to share price will occur; it will be a STV when confidence level is

moderate. A Catalyst Watch or STV Upside/Downside call will automatically expire at the end of the specified 30/90 day
period. The Catalyst Watch will also be automatically removed if share price performance (calculated at market close) exceeds
15% against the direction of the call (unless over-ridden by the analyst). The analyst may also remove a Catalyst Watch or STV
call prior to the end of the specified period in a published research note. A Catalyst Watch/STV Upside or Downside call may be

2Q26 result overview 
NYPCB 2Q26 core business (OPI) was 15% higher than BBG 
consensus, but was 8% lower than our estimate (most bullish on the 
street). GM went up by 8.9ppt QoQ, the highest growth among all 
Taiwan ABF players (Unimicron/Kinsus’ GM went up by 
+6.8ppt/+4.9ppt QoQ only in 2Q26), which is in-line with our more 
bullish view on spot price ABF substrates players’ profitability 
expansion speed vs. LTA players. For earnings, NYPCB’s 2Q EPS was 
5% higher than the BBG consensus but was 18% lower than GSe.  
3Q26 outlook - UTR in Kunshan start ramping up despite low yield 
rate, while ABF/BT pricing hike will continue to drive GM/OPM 
For 3Q26, we expect NYPCB’s revenue will go up by 27% QoQ in 
3Q26, while the key update is the company’s Kunshan plant 
(~15-20% of overall ABF substrate capacity) utilization rate reached 
70% in July (vs. 50% in 2Q26; which drove July revenue went up by 
16% MoM), with key high end customers starting to place orders in 
mainland China (which is in-line with our view that all the ABF 
substrates capacity will be working under fully utilized level from 
2H26, due to the severe shortage condition of ~15-20% shortage 
ratio; see here). 
However, our understanding is that the company’s Kunshan plant 
was built for mid to low end ABF substrates, and the increasing 
production on high-end ABF substrate in Kunshan plant could lead 
to an unfavorable GM for overall ABF substrates business in the near 
term (3Q26), but we do expect this will be offset by the increasing 
spot price from August/September, and we also believe the yield 
rate will eventually improve and lead to an even higher than 
company’s average GM in coming months/quarters. As a result, we 
expect NYPCB’s 3Q GM will further jump by 6.6ppt QoQ to 
31.3%, whose GM could surpass Kinsus/Unimicron and become 
the highest among all Taiwan ABF suppliers (we expect

Kinsus/Unimicron GM will reach 28.4%/27.4% in 3Q).  
Investment thesis - GM to surpass TW peers from 2H26; new CAPEX on high end ABF 
substrates suggesting more upside on ASP in LT 
As discussed several times (see here), we believe NYPCB will start outgrowing players on 
the margin side from 2H26, when the ABF substrate market enters a real supply 
shortage stage, and we saw NYPCB’s GM approaching Kinsus/Unimicron’s in 2Q26, who 
should start delivering a stronger GM from 2H26, as we discussed, due mainly to the 
company’s proactive strategy on pricing (focus on spot price market should make the 
company deliver a better GM/OPM outlook than LTA peers during the shortage period). 
Considering our positive view on the ABF substrate demand and the long capacity 
expansion lead time of the industry, we believe the ABF substrate capacity shortage 
period will last for 30+ months, implying NYPCB’s outperformance on GM/OPM growth 
vs. other TW ABF names should not be just a short-term condition.  
Moreover, the company announced a new CAPEX plan (here), while the company will 
invest in NT$46.8bn (vs. the company’s overall CAPEX in 2025 was only at NT$2.4bn vs. 
peak annual CAPEX in last upcycle was NT$16.9bn only) on a new high-end ABF 
substrate plant in Chiayi. Based on our estimate, the new capacity will be used to 
produce TSMC next generation CoPoS packaging substrates, and the capacity could 
come on stream right after TSMC start mass producing CoPoS packaging technology in 
2H28-1H29 (the capacity expansion lead time is also in-line with our view of 30+ 
months). We do believe the company’s proactive investment in the next generation 
packaging technology should lead the company’s overall ASP to another level, while we 
believe the substrate size / layer count could be 100%/ 30%+ larger/higher than the 
company’s most high end products now, suggesting a long term revenue/earnings 
outlook after 2028.  
Overall, considering the near term profitability outperformance and the positive 
long term revenue/earning outlook with the new high-end capacity expansion, we 
maintain our Buy rating (on CL) on NYPCB with new TP of NT$2,500 (from 
NT$2,310).

Earnings revision 
We revise up our 2027/28/29E earnings estimate by 2/9/23% to factor in the more

favorable than expected ABF GM outlook in coming years considering the more

We are Buy rated on NYPCB, with a new TP of NT$2,500 (from NT$2,310), based on an 
unchanged 21x 2028E EPS (+1.5x STDV above the past upcycle average P/E of 13.3x as 
we expect a better pricing outlook in spot price market this time vs. last upcycle in 
2020-2022), considering the much better customer mix and the much better demand 
visibility.

Investment Thesis & PT methodology and risks

Investment Thesis - NYPCB 
NYPCB manufactures and markets printed circuit boards (PCBs) and integrated circuit 
(IC) substrates (including ABF and BT substrates). We are Buy-rated on NYPCB 
considering the company’s high exposure to BT substrates and non-LTA ABF substrate

### Page 5

(70%+ of total revenue), where we expect GM/OPM to increase significantly in the
coming quarters (from breakeven in OPM in 1H25 to 27/41% in 2026/27E) due to the
favorable substrate pricing outlook. Despite the company’s low exposure to high-end
ABF substrate industry (<20% of total revenue in 1H25), we believe the expanding
demand from ASIC AI server and high-end switch IC demand will drive the company’s
high-end ABF revenue exposure to 40%+ by 2027E, suggesting better GM/OPM outlook

in the long term. In 2H25-2H26, we believe NYPCB will continue to benefit from stronger
ABF/BT pricing and should be a key beneficiary of the pricing uptrend considering the
company’s key focus on BT and non-LTA ABF substrate industries. Moreover, in the long
term (after 2026), we expect the ABF industry to return to supply tightness, and NYPCB’s
expanding high-end exposure will lead to a long term earnings growth outlook (122%
2026-28E earnings CAGR). As a result, we see solid upside potential for NYPCB’s

earnings and stock performance.
Price Target Risks and Methodology - NYPCB
Valuation methodology: Our 12-month target price of NT$2,500 for NYPCB is based
on 21x 2028E P/E, which is +1.5x STDV higher than the past upcycle average P/E (13.3x).
Key downside risks: (1) Slower-than-expected PC demand recovery, (2)
Slower-than-expected ABF substrates pricing upgrading outlook, and (3)
Slower-than-expected NYPCB new high-end capacity qualification progress.

Ratings, coverage universe and related definitions
Buy (B), Neutral (N), Sell (S) Analysts recommend stocks as Buys or Sells for inclusion on various regional Investment Lists. Being assigned a Buy or Sell
on an Investment List is determined by a stock’s total return potential relative to its coverage universe. Any stock not assigned as a Buy or a Sell on an
Investment List with an active rating (i.e., a  stock that is not Rating Suspended, Not Rated, Early-Stage Biotech, Coverage Suspended or Not Covered), is

deemed Neutral. Each region manages Regional Conviction Lists, which are selected from Buy rated stocks on the respective region’s Investment Lists
and represent investment recommendations focused on the size of the total return potential and/or the likelihood of the realization of the return across
their respective areas of coverage.  The addition or removal of stocks from such Conviction Lists are managed by the Investment Review Committee or
other designated committee in each respective region and do not represent a change in the analysts’ investment rating for such stocks.

Total return potential represents the upside or downside differential between the current share price and the price target, including all paid or
anticipated dividends, expected during the time horizon associated with the price target. Price targets are required for all covered stocks. The total
return potential, price target and associated time horizon are stated in each report adding or reiterating an Investment List membership.

Novatek  | Asia Pacific
3Q Revenue Growth Led by
Smartphones
2Q results beat: Revenue rose 24% Q/Q, mainly from early pull-in demand, with
SoC now 50% of total revenue. The GM increased 3.4ppt Q/Q, to 42.4%, supported
by price increases, favorable mix and low-cost inventory.
3Q revenue up 4.7-11.7% Q/Q; margin slightly lower at 38.5-41.5%: Management
expects smartphone demand to grow significantly in 3Q, as Apple demand remains
resilient. SoC revenue should be flat Q/Q, as machine vision edge AI demand offsets

LDDI-related SoC weakness. LDDI will  decline Q/Q owing to weaker demand and
panel makers' decreasing production. GM may decline with an unfavorable mix, but
Novatek will likely raise prices again to reflect the cost burden. The OpM will be 19-
20%.
Highlights from today's call:
• Auto: Expects strong Y/Y growth given share gain.
• Foundry/OSAT price hike: Continues to see elevated upstream costs into
2027, passing them through to customers or optimizing design.

Stay UW: We expect the GM to remain resilient at current levels, but valuation is
full  at 15x 2027e EPS, while the company's exposure to AI is limited.

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.
Unless otherwise specified, the time frame for price targets included in Morgan Stanley Research is 12 to 18 months.
Analyst Industry Views
Attractive (A): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be attractive vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated
below.
In-Line (I): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be in line with the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

Cautious (C): The analyst views the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months with caution vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.
Benchmarks for each region are as follows: North America - S&P 500; Latin America - relevant MSCI country index or MSCI Latin America Index; Europe - MSCI Europe; Japan - TOPIX; Asia -
relevant MSCI country index or MSCI sub-regional index or MSCI AC Asia Pacific ex Japan Index.

WT Microelectronics Co. Ltd.  | Asia Pacific
Stronger 2H driven by data
center and communication; OW
Flat QoQ revenue outlook but stronger 2H: Management guided for NT$572-612bn
revenue, flat QoQ, up 80% YoY, mainly due to the pace of data-center-related
shipments. Management expects 2H to be stronger than 1H and data center to
continue its strong growth in 2027. 3Q EPS is guided at NT$7.55-8.52; the mid-point
is 41%/26% higher than MSe/consensus; the outlook suggests continued benefits

from operating leverage and a more favorable business mix.
2Q26 earnings beat on AI-driven scale and operating leverage: 2Q26  revenue
reached NT$591bn (+20% QoQ, +128% YoY), operating margin expanded to 2.20%
despite gross margin edging down slightly  to 3.41%; EPS was NT$7.68 (+44% QoQ,
+237% YoY), 3%/21% higher than MSe/consensus. By segment, datacenter & server
remained the key growth engine, accounting for 59% of revenue and posting 24%
QoQ / 274% YoY growth.

Maintain OW: WT Micro continues to gain share in AI infrastructure while
maintaining disciplined cost control; the stock trades at 8.3x  2027e EPS, below it
8.9x average one-year forward P/E since 2018; we view the valuation as attractive,
given the company's continued progress in penetrating the AI supply chain.

n Margin trends up even amid low-margin AI business.
n Global semi inventory drops, reflecting strong mobile device demand.
n Market share expands.

Risks to Downside
n Margin trends down dramatically amid slower-than-expected AI-semi development.
n Global semi inventory rises, reflecting poor mobile/PC device demand.
n Market share lost.

STOCK RATINGS
Morgan Stanley uses a relative rating system using terms such as Overweight, Equal-weight, Not-Rated or Underweight (see definitions below). Morgan Stanley does not assign ratings of Buy,
Hold or Sell to the stocks we cover. Overweight, Equal-weight, Not-Rated and Underweight are not the equivalent of buy, hold and sell.  Investors should carefully read the definitions of all
ratings used in Morgan Stanley Research. In addition, since Morgan Stanley Research contains more complete information concerning the analyst's views, investors should carefully read Morgan

Stanley Research, in its entirety, and not infer the contents from the rating alone.  In any case, ratings (or research) should not be used or relied upon as investment advice.  An investor's decision
to buy or sell a stock should depend on individual circumstances (such as the investor's existing holdings) and other considerations.

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.
Unless otherwise specified, the time frame for price targets included in Morgan Stanley Research is 12 to 18 months.
Analyst Industry Views
Attractive (A): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be attractive vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated
below.
In-Line (I): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be in line with the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

Cautious (C): The analyst views the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months with caution vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.
Benchmarks for each region are as follows: North America - S&P 500; Latin America - relevant MSCI country index or MSCI Latin America Index; Europe - MSCI Europe; Japan - TOPIX; Asia -
relevant MSCI country index or MSCI sub-regional index or MSCI AC Asia Pacific ex Japan Index.

### Page 1

ab 6 August 2026
Global Research
King Slide Works
AI king  delivered record sales/margins
Q226 New record set for margins
Q226 EPS  of NT$74.38 was well ahead of UBSe/consensus, highlighted by record GM of
87.4% (+~10ppts QoQ/YoY) w/ OPM expanded to 82.1% (+12-15ppts QoQ/YoY). We
believe the revenue outperformance was driven by strong pull-ins for existing and new
products  with favourable product mix and increased complexity driving the significant

margin expansion. King Slide also reported July sales of NT$6.4bn (+44% MoM, +356%
YoY), reaching 65% of Street Q326 sales estimates of NT$9.9bn. We await more color
and guidance from its earnings call on August 7th.
Profitability level is key from here
While we believe King Slide has benefited from some AI upcycle elements (expanding
TAM on increasing computing and networking/storage/power intensity with strong
content value in AI server infrastructure), we equally believe that 1) there have been

several underlying structural improvements from its efforts to develop in-house
automated and manufacturing equipment to achieve low-cost and high-efficiency
production; 2) there are more upcycle positives to come as rail kit demand and
complexity extend from single compute rack to AI POD level with a wide variety of
customized specifications; and 3) the company is becoming more dominant in the
ecosystem to define next-generation AI server system and infrastructure. We believe

management's original long-term gross margin guidance of 75% +/-1-2% (which is still
impressive, in our view)  could be proven to be  conservative, and lift our GM assumptions
from 76%/76%/76% to 80%/77%/77% in 2026/2027/2028E.
Stellar growth  story continues
We have been highlighting rail kit content value to more than double from Hopper HGX
to Vera Rubin rack with further growth upside to be captured from other purpose-built
rack-scale systems that call for specialized rail kits. Some investors were concerned on

increasing competition; yet growth upside surprises in recent months suggest pull-ins
for prior/current generation from both CSPs and enterprises were stronger than
expected, signalling evident technology gap and market leadership remains intact. The
company is ramping up US fab production in Q326, with King Slide Fab 2 expected to
come online in 2H27 while H126 capex remain disciplined at c.3% of total sales. We
now forecast King Slide to deliver a 65% revenue CAGR over 2025-28E (vs.  prior 37%).

Valuation: Lift price  target to NT$14,000; maintain Buy
We reiterate our Buy rating as the company remains solidly positioned to ride on strong
AI infrastructure builds with evident content gains boosted by design complexity and its
dominant market position. Q226 and July results' solid beat reassures its strong growth/
margin profile with constructive outlook ahead. We lift our 2026/2027/2028 EPS
estimates by  66%/73%/75% and raise our PT to NT$14,000 (30x 2028 PE, down from

prior 33x but  in line with AI component leaders  at 25-35x consensus P/E).

### Page 2

King Slide Works UBS Research
Thesis Map     UBS                    Research     THESIS MAP a guide to our thinking and what´s where in this report
Q: Can King Slide maintain a leading AI server rail kit share  and deliver a double-digit sales
CAGR?
Yes. We expect King Slide to maintain its market share  in the next three to five years due to its: 1)
proven track record in product quality and reliability; and 2) scale benefits and accumulated patents,

enabling strong ecosystem collaboration. Rapid technological advancements in next-generation AI
servers with much more complex designs make it challenging for competitors to narrow the
technology gap with King Slide. Given its leading position and exposure that is highly geared to
hyperscalers, we think King Slide will benefit from the AI upcycle and deliver strong sales momentum,
with a  65% CAGR in 2025-28E.
Q: Can King Slide sustain GM at 70%+ in upcoming years?

Likely. We anticipate King Slide's GM to remain relatively stable, at 75%+ in 2025-28E. Increasing
component density and system complexity are lifting the  entry barrier and the large number of server-
related patents King Slide has creates a strong moat for stable margins. Also, rail kits account for less
than 1% of AI server BOM cost, making them unlikely to encounter pricing pressure from customers.
We expect King Slide's sales to outperform server ODM sales growth and overall server unit growth in

the next few years due to its: 1) higher exposure to hyperscalers, which are outgrowing the server
market; 2) rising content from increasing component density and system complexity; and 3) solid
market position, supported by its robust patents, technological innovation and relationships in the
ecosystem.
Our analysis of King Slide's AI server rail kit revenue suggests it has more than a 70% market share,
while AI server rail kits accounted for 60-70% of its total server revenue in H126, a sharp rise from

7-8% in H123. In terms of server rail kit content, we estimate its content value will increase from cUS
$1,400 per rack for HGX to  US$2,500+ for GB300 and US$3,000+ for Vera Rubin. We foresee
further content upside with expansion into POD-scale AI platforms, where multiple configurations
and design complexity  may call for additional server rail kit content. We think complexity will increase
further with blade configurations in NVIDIA’s next Kyber architecture.

The stock is trading at 34x/25x/22x 2026E/2027E/2028E PE, at the peak of its valuation range.  Our
price target implies 30x 2028E PE with a 63% earnings CAGR in 2025-28E. We believe this multiple is
justified, as we think it may trade at premiums similar to those of other AI component leaders, such as
Delta/Jentech (25x/35x one-year forward P/E, respectively), warranted by its technological leadership
and structural margin advantages.

King Slide is one of the world's largest slide and rail kit solution providers for servers, along with
industrial and consumer electronics applications.  It tapped into server rails in 2001 after securing its

first order and has extended relationships with  enterprise servers  and major CSPs. As of H125, server
rail kits accounted for 80-90% of its total sales, with 10-20% from kitchen/home furniture fittings.

UPSIDE (NT$18,500): We assume 2028E total revenue of NT$93.4B with server rail kits
revenue to grow at 30% YoY and non server rail kits (including kitchen rail kit, slides, and
hinges, etc) revenue to grow at 9% YoY in 2028E. We assume net margins of 60% to
arrive at 2028E EPS of NT$585. We then apply a target multiple of ~32x to arrive at our
NT$18,500 upside valuation.
BASE (NT$14,000): We assume 2028E total revenue of NT$78.3B with server rail kits

revenue to grow at 17% YoY and non server rail kits (including kitchen rail kit, slides, and
hinges, etc) revenue to grow at 4% YoY in 2028E, and we assume net margins of 57% to
arrive at 2028E EPS of NT$470. We then apply a target multiple of ~30x to arrive at our
NT$14,000 price target.
DOWNSIDE (NT$6,000): We assume 2028E total revenue of NT$52.1B with server rail
kits revenue to grow at 8% YoY and non server rail kits (including kitchen rail kit, slides,

and hinges, etc) revenue to grow at -1% YoY in 2028E. We assume net margins of 55%
to arrive at 2028E EPS of NT$300. We then apply a target multiple of ~20x to arrive at our
NT$6,000 downside valuation.

Company Description
King Slide is one of the world's largest slide and rail kit solution providers for servers, along
with industrial and consumer electronic applications. Established as a furniture hardware
manufacturer, King Slide tapped into server rails 2001 after securing its first order and has

further extended relationships with: 1) enterprise server names HPE, IBM and Dell; and 2)
major CSPs Amazon, Microsoft, Google, Meta and Oracle, etc. As of H125, server rail kits
accounted for 80-90% of its total sales, with 10-20% from kitchen/home furniture fittings.
Valuation Method and Risk Statement
We derive our price target for King Slide using PE valuation, relative to its historical trading
range.Key downside risks include: 1) weaker-than-expected server demand, particularly from

hyperscalers; 2) market share losses or fiercer industry competition; 3) slower-than-expected
server platform upgrades; and 4) lower-than-expected addressable content within servers.

the one-year local market interest rate plus 5% (a proxy for, and not a forecast of, the equity risk premium). Under Review (UR)
Stocks may be flagged as UR by the analyst, indicating that the stock's price target and/or rating are subject to possible change in the

near term, usually in response to an event that may affect the investment case or valuation. Equity Price Targets have an investment
horizon of 12 months.

Investing in structured investments involves significant risks. For a detailed discussion of the risks involved in investing in any particular structured investment, you must read

the relevant offering materials for that investment. Structured investments are unsecured obligations of a particular issuer with returns linked to the performance of an
underlying asset. Depending on the terms of the investment, investors could lose all or a substantial portion of their investment based on the performance of the underlying
asset. Investors could also lose their entire investment if the issuer becomes insolvent. UBS does not guarantee in any way the obligations or the financial condition of any

issuer or the accuracy of any financial information provided by any issuer. Structured investments are not traditional investments and investing in a structured investment is
not equivalent to investing directly in the underlying asset. Structured investments may have limited or no liquidity, and investors should be prepared to hold their investment
to maturity. The return of structured investments may be limited by a maximum gain, participation rate or other feature. Structured investments may include call features and,

if a structured investment is called early, investors would not earn any further return and may not be able to reinvest in similar investments with similar terms. Structured
investments include costs and fees which are generally embedded in the price of the investment. The tax treatment of a structured investment may be complex and may differ
from a direct investment in the underlying asset. UBS and its employees do not provide tax advice. Investors should consult their own tax advisor about their own tax situation

before investing in any securities.

would be consistent with its investment objective and other principal investment strategies. The returns on a portfolio incorporating ESG factors or sustainable investing
considerations may be lower or higher than portfolios where ESG factors, exclusions, or other sustainability issues are not considered by the portfolio manager, and the
investment opportunities available to such portfolios may differ.

Update
Gold Circuit Electronics Ltd.  | Asia Pacific
July Sales up 21% M/M and 77%
Y/Y
We attribute the July revenue beat to:
• Ramp of Trainium 3 UBB PCB shipments, which started in late 2Q.
• Additional capacity coming online in Taiwan, Thailand and Suzhou, China.
• Continued pricing adjustments with customers.
• Potentially some delayed shipments from June.
July revenue reached 33% and 35% of and our consensus 3Q revenue estimates,
respectively, which implies upside to our and the Street's 3Q forecasts, as the ramp

of Trainium 3 PCB and increasing capacity could drive monthly revenue up m/m
throughout 3Q.
Stay OW:  The stock is currently trading at just 11.9x 2027e and 9.0x 2028e P/E,
which we view as attractive vs. its three-year forward P/E range of 7x to 25x and its
Chinese peers at 30x+. Stay OW.
GCE is scheduled to report 2Q26 results on August 11, after the market closes.

Key Takeaways
GCE reported July sales of NT$10.0bn (+21% m/m, +77% y/y), 17% above MSe.
GCE's July revenue was ahead of MSe and the Street, accounting for 33% and
35% of MSe and consensus estimates of 3Q26 revenues, respectively.
With new project ramp (T3), we believe monthly revenue should remain elevated
or grow m/m, so there should be upside to both MSe and Street.

ratings used in Morgan Stanley Research. In addition, since Morgan Stanley Research contains more complete information concerning the analyst's views, investors should carefully read Morgan

Stanley Research, in its entirety, and not infer the contents from the rating alone.  In any case, ratings (or research) should not be used or relied upon as investment advice.  An investor's decision
to buy or sell a stock should depend on individual circumstances (such as the investor's existing holdings) and other considerations.

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.
Unless otherwise specified, the time frame for price targets included in Morgan Stanley Research is 12 to 18 months.
Analyst Industry Views
Attractive (A): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be attractive vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated
below.
In-Line (I): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be in line with the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

Cautious (C): The analyst views the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months with caution vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.
Benchmarks for each region are as follows: North America - S&P 500; Latin America - relevant MSCI country index or MSCI Latin America Index; Europe - MSCI Europe; Japan - TOPIX; Asia -
relevant MSCI country index or MSCI sub-regional index or MSCI AC Asia Pacific ex Japan Index.

### Page 1

Taiwan Information Technology

What’s new: ITEQ released 2Q26 results after market hours on 7 August.

What’s the impact: 2Q26 results beat on stronger-than-expected 
gross margin. 2Q26 revenue grew 26% QoQ to TWD11.5bn mainly due to 
a solid price hike of 30% with a stable utilisation rate of 70-75%. 2Q26 net 
income of TWD1,279m (EPS: TWD3.52) was 71-118% higher than our 
estimate (TWD587m) and the consensus (TWD750m) due to a stronger-
than-expected gross margin. Gross margin expanded to 10.6pp to 22% in 
2Q26, while operating margin increased 11.4pp to 16% due to decent opex 
control. Net income was also boosted by a slightly lower tax rate of 30.2% 
(vs. 35% in 1Q26). In 2Q26, ITEQ had 60% of total sales from regular 
servers, with 10% from AI servers and 400G switches, per our estimates.

Business outlook. We believe the CCL industry is experiencing a 
favourable ASP trend from 2Q26 given that AI servers have continuously 
crowded out most of the tier-1 CCL capacities, as capacities are shifted 
from non-AI to AI to meet end demand. This has caused mid-to-low end 
orders to spill over to the next tranche that is currently benefiting E-glass 
vendors including ITEQ. We believe the E-glass price rose more than 80% 
in 1H26, which caused vendors to raise their ASP by 30% to pass on the 
cost to end customers. Looking ahead, we expect ITEQ to deliver revenue 
growth of c.20% QoQ in 3Q26 and 10% QoQ in 4Q26 as we expect it to 
keep passing on additional costs from E-glass price hikes (5-10% per 
month as of now). We believe these price hikes will continue into 1H27; in 
our view, the price hike impact from 2026 plus a likely 20% hike in 2027 
would cause 2027 revenue to grow 43% without a meaningful capacity 
increase. ITEQ today announced a new capacity expansion plan in Wuxi, 
which will likely increase its capacity by 2.85m. With the additional new 
capacity from Thailand (600k in 2028), its capacity will increase from 5.45m 
sheets in 2026E to 6.05m/8m/8.9m in 2027-2029E. Its capacity ramp-up is 
targeting high-end CCL demand from hyperscalers from 2028 onward.

What we recommend: We lift our 2026-28E EPS by 111-206% on price 
hikes. We lift our 12M TP to TWD600 (from TWD333), based on a target 
PER of 22x (higher than the range of its past-1-year range of 8-18x), applied 
to our 1-year-forward EPS forecast. We lower our PER target from 30x as we 
believe upside from price hikes has been partially priced in. Nonetheless, we 
upgrade the stock to Buy (1) from Outperform (2), as we believe ITEQ will 
gradually benefit from high-end CCL demand given the current supply-
demand situation. Key risk: weaker-than-expected regular server demand.

How we differ: Our 2026-27E EPS are 66-180% above consensus, mainly 
due to our more aggressive view on ITEQ’s revenue and margin profile.

7 August 2026

Upgrading: strong beat in 2Q26 with improving outlook

➢ Gross margin expanded significantly on favourable pricing trend  
➢ Massive capacity expansion indicates a resolution to tap into AI servers  
➢ Upgrading to Buy (1) rating; raising our 12-month TP to TWD600

Company profile 
ITEQ Corporation is a leading manufacturer of high performance copper clad laminate materials used 
for the fabrication of printed circuit boards and was founded in April of 1997. ITEQ’s products find use 
many different application areas, such as computational and communications applications, Radio 
Frequency and Microwave devices, Automotive applications, and High Density Interconnect solutions.

### Page 1

ab 8 August 2026
Global Research
First Read
Giant Manufacturing
Q226 beat on stronger gross margin; positive
sales outlook intact
Q: How did the results compare with expectations?
In Q226, Giant reported net profit up 234% YoY, 126%/28% above the UBS/consensus
estimates. The beat was mainly due to a higher-than-expected gross margin.
Q: What were the most noteworthy areas in the results?
In Q226, sales  rose 6% YoY. Sales in China were up 13% YoY, ending its declining trend

of the past six consecutive quarters. Sales contraction in Europe/US narrowed to
4%/15%. E-bike sales also resumed growth at 20% YoY. Newly launched city e-bikes in
Europe  were well received and drove strong volume growth. Gross margin expanded
4.4ppt YoY on new model launches and a higher portion of own-brand sales (higher
profitability than OEM orders). Opex came in higher than we expected due to marketing
expenses for new product launches, legal fees for its WRO (Withhold  Release Order) and

sports sponsorships (earlier this year vs. in Q3 previously).
Q: Has the company's guidance/outlook changed?
No. Management kept its  2026 top-line guidance unchanged at negative low-
single digits, with the operating margin at 1-3% despite the strong Q226 results.
Management continues to see a positive trend in Europe/China early in Q326 and  initial
signs of a recovery in the US. However, management  is keeping conservative guidance,
considering  macro risks.

Q: How do we expect investors to react?
We think the market is likely to react positively to the results beat.  We raise our
2026E earnings 47% to factor in the Q226 results beat and  better-than-expected
profitability.   We reiterate our Buy rating, based on our positive sector view, and we most
prefer Giant among our covered stocks in this sector. Our price target remains
unchanged at NT$126, based on 17x 2027E PE.

Company Description
Giant Manufacturing (Giant) is the world's largest bicycle manufacturer by volume. It was
established in 1972 and was listed on Taiwan's main board in 1994. Giant focuses on mid- to
high-end bicycle models, with the original brand manufacturing (OBM) business making up

61% of sales and the rest coming from original design manufacturing (ODM) clients as of
Q125.
Valuation Method and Risk Statement
Our price target is based on a PE multiple.
The key downside risks are: 1) a global economic slowdown impacting end-user demand; 2)
foreign exchange risk; 3) a rise in raw material prices; 4) unfavourable weather; and 5) lower-
than-expected popularity of e-bike products.

Investing in structured investments involves significant risks. For a detailed discussion of the risks involved in investing in any particular structured investment, you must read

the relevant offering materials for that investment. Structured investments are unsecured obligations of a particular issuer with returns linked to the performance of an
underlying asset. Depending on the terms of the investment, investors could lose all or a substantial portion of their investment based on the performance of the underlying
asset. Investors could also lose their entire investment if the issuer becomes insolvent. UBS does not guarantee in any way the obligations or the financial condition of any

issuer or the accuracy of any financial information provided by any issuer. Structured investments are not traditional investments and investing in a structured investment is
not equivalent to investing directly in the underlying asset. Structured investments may have limited or no liquidity, and investors should be prepared to hold their investment
to maturity. The return of structured investments may be limited by a maximum gain, participation rate or other feature. Structured investments may include call features and,

if a structured investment is called early, investors would not earn any further return and may not be able to reinvest in similar investments with similar terms. Structured
investments include costs and fees which are generally embedded in the price of the investment. The tax treatment of a structured investment may be complex and may differ
from a direct investment in the underlying asset. UBS and its employees do not provide tax advice. Investors should consult their own tax advisor about their own tax situation

before investing in any securities.

considerations may be lower or higher than portfolios where ESG factors, exclusions, or other sustainability issues are not considered by the portfolio manager, and the
investment opportunities available to such portfolios may differ.

Key observations:
1. The ROIC of AI capex is the most important thing to track for future
hardware demand
2. While spending continues, how big the "memory cake" should be is a
growing debate
3. The implied chip volume vs. electricity consumption (GW) was frequently
asked about, suggesting that power supply remains a global bottleneck

4. Memory currently still attracts more attention than logic/computing semis
5. Within memory, we see a split in participation between momentum and
value investors
6. What are key stocks to own, if  investors' focus shifts from pricing hike to
volume play?

• AI investments  are paying off, according to  CSPs' official comments

communications with a subject company, public appearances
and trading securities held by a research analyst account.

Some investors' interests are moving to "volume plays" if "price hike" momentum
slows down.

Key observations on HK investors' positioning and interests
The ROIC of AI capex is the most important thing to track for
future hardware demand
For Greater China Semis, our major underlying assumptions for AI semis come from cloud
capex updates ( Exhibit 5 ) and major CSPs' narratives on RoI (Exhibit 2 ). Some HK
investors told us that they are tracking the ARR of LLM vendors, on a quarterly or even
monthly basis, to make sure the revenue expansion is fast enough to cover the growing

capex costs. We alluded to Brian Nowak’s recent research: Internet: The Paths to 25-50%
GenAI ROIC (27 Jul 2026). And from the recent rise in cloud capex budgets, AI revenue
growth, as well as the positive narratives on CSPs' ROIC, we are not worried about
overproduction of AI GPUs and ASICs in 2027 –  our assessment of TSMC CoWoS capacity
implies a 70%-80% Y/Y increase in chip output in 2027.
While spending continues, how big the "memory cake"
should be is a growing debate

Some investors even believe that if memory’s portion of cloud capex budgets shifts to
computing and networking chips, AI capex may generate more computing capacity.  In our
cloud capex 2027 breakdown analysis ( Exhibit 5 ), memory accounts for 53% of the
budget of US$1.5tn, while computing (GPUs, ASICs, CPUs) accounts for 20%.
Exhibit 1: 2027 Cloud Capex Breakdown by Using US$1.5 Trillion as the Total  Budget

The implied chip volume vs. electricity consumption (GW)
was frequently asked about, suggesting that power supply
remains a global bottleneck
When we showed our 2027 CoWoS implied chip volume (around 19 mn units), investors
were concerned about whether there would be sufficient electricity.  If each GPU/ASIC's

average TDP is 2kw, it would mean that around 38GW of new electricity would be
needed. Looking at it the other way around, when SpaceX indicated that it would expand
its capacity from 2GW by this year-end, to closer to 10GW as an ambitious target  (see
SpaceX: Strong Beat/Outlook vs. Higher Capex), we were also asked what that means to
TSMC’s CoWoS capacity consumption. Of note, Adam Jonas is only modeling in 3 GW of
incremental computing – getting to 5 GW by year-end 2027. But just in a rough sense, the

additional 8GW would mean around 4mn units of Rubin GPUs or 20% of the AI
accelerators that TSMC produces in 2027. See Exhibit 5  and Exhibit 2  for detailed
analysis.

Memory currently still attracts more attention than logic/

computing semis
HK investors were still more interests in the memory stocks than the logic foundries, such
as TSMC, ASE, and MediaTek. We believe it is because of more positioning with more
volatility in memory stocks. Some investors even stated a belief that “if memory stocks
don’t work, logic foundries like TSMC also can’t work.” Historically, the logic semi and
memory cycles  were indeed concurrent, if they shared the same end demand. However,

we can sense that this time around, there are memory-specific debates. For example, by
sharing the same AI infrastructure, memory is facing more “spec change.” For example, we
were cross-checked about the Rubin Ultra’s HBM spec change and the new Apple iPhone's
DRAM density being reduced from 12GB to 9GB. It seems that investors are trying to

### Page 5

M
Idea
Morgan Stanley Research 5
figure out whether those spec changes have been caused by shortage or customers’ intent
to save costs.
Within memory, we see a split in participation between
momentum and value investors
In our Asian Tech strategist Shawn Kim’s report Asia Technology: Memory – A Small
Wrinkle (6 Aug 2026), he focused on the memory trade vs. valuation support. “The
sharpest correction in the memory industry to date appears to have ended, shifting focus

to buybacks as the next catalyst. ” We do think that momentum investors in HK are more
conservative on the memory stock price rebound – they are focusing on secondary
derivatives (eg. DRAM price Y/Y change), earnings estimate revision breadth, and spot
market trend. For example, when we gave our view about potential price moderation in
Chinese consumer NAND modules into 4Q26, some investors were concerned that it
may affect contract prices in 2027.

However, another camp of memory investors really appreciates the potential cash returns
from memory companies. They are willing to use P/E multiples to evaluate the stocks and
buy on recent dips.
What are key stocks to own, if  investors' focus shifts from
pricing hike to volume play?
We are still bullish on commodity semis such as DRAM/NAND and MLCC. However, some
momentum investors expect  consolidation. Instead, they turned their focus back to

volume plays or  business models, technology that can also add value or increase the
efficiency of AI infrastructure.
Key OWs in logic/compute
• We think TSMC’s wafer business and ASE’s packaging (along with ABF substrates)
are the key concepts of volume plays. Otherwise, we continue to like the ASIC
story (saving on budgets if successfully executed) and keep MediaTek as our Top
Pick.
• We also pushed the agenetic AI enablers CPU proxies in Greater China semis,

including Aspeed (server BMC), Hygon (Chinese CPU), GUC (Google Arm-based
CPU), and ASE (AMD server CPU).
Our calls in niche memories
• Within niche memory, we prefer DDR4 DRAM (OW Nanya Tech and Winbond) to
NAND module makers (EW Phison and Longsys).
Stocks to avoid
• We also suggest avoiding low-end commodity players that may not be able to hike
prices successfully – for example, GWC (EW rated) in raw wafers and Silergy (UW
rated) in consumer analog.

Key analysis: TSMC CoWoS vs. GW consumption
How significant is SpaceX's 8GW data center build in 2027?
During its  earnings call on August 4, SpaceX announced an exclusive commitment to build
its future computing capacity on Nvidia's Vera Rubin architecture. The scale is
unprecedented: 2 gigawatts (GW) of installed computing by 2026, scaling to around 10
GW by 2027.
If realized, this would represent a substantial expansion in SpaceX’s computing footprint

and could support increasingly computing-intensive AI workloads across its businesses.
The buildout would also create another sizable demand vector for NVIDIA accelerators
and the broader AI infrastructure supply chain, including memory, logic wafer supply,
networking, power, etc.
If we assume that all of the 8GW of computing will be Vera Rubin in 2027, it would
suggest around 400k of CoWoS wafer demand, accounting for close to half of Nvidia
Rubin's CoWoS booking in 2027. According to our latest CoWoS forecast, we are expecting

around 38GW of computing power to be produced from TSMC, while SpaceX's 8GW plan
could account for around 20% of total GPU/ASIC production.

Global CSPs kept raising capex during C2Q26 earnings
CSP capex continues to move higher as hyperscalers lean into AI infrastructure buildout
amid sustained demand and ongoing capacity constraints. Recent guidance revisions from
Amazon, Alphabet, and Meta point to a higher-for-longer investment cycle, while
Microsoft’s underlying infrastructure spending remains elevated despite accounting-
related changes in reported capex.
The key investor debate is increasingly shifting from whether AI demand is real to whether

incremental returns can keep pace with the magnitude of capital deployment. For now,
management commentary suggests that confidence remains high, with capacity largely
being absorbed as it comes online, but the rising spending profile raises the bar for future
cloud growth, AI monetization, and free cash flow conversion.
AI investments  are paying off, according to  CSPs' official
comments

### Page 7

M
Idea
Morgan Stanley Research 7
During those CSPs' earnings calls, commentary continued to support the view that rising
AI capex is increasingly underwritten by visible monetization rather than purely
speculative demand. Below are key comments from those CSPs.
Meta: Mark Zuckerberg said “these AI investments are paying off” in the core ads business.
More importantly for the forward ROI debate, he argued that there should be “a
significantly higher margin on selling intelligence rather than selling compute directly. But

think that there's a big opportunity obviously to sell compute as well" and "quite
optimistic to see meaningful growth in all of these areas."
Google: On the capex/ROI question, CFO Anat Ashkenazi said the objective is to “invest as
long as we see an attractive return on that investment.” CEO Pichai added that Alphabet
has become more bullish on the AI opportunity over the past year, citing stronger
consumer, enterprise and developer momentum.
Amazon: Amazon disclosed that AWS's AI revenue run-rate is now above US$25bn and

growing at triple-digit rates YoY, after rising significantly QoQ. Amazon also said that AI
and traditional/core AWS workloads are increasingly reinforcing each other's growth.
Microsoft:- CFO Amy Hood said, “Customer demand continues to exceed available
capacity” and that incremental Azure capacity was “quickly monetized.”
Exhibit 6: Consensus CY27 capex just increased by 70%+ (or ~$500bn) YTD, which makes us believe that as long as the qualitative

commentary on cloud spending stays robust, there likely will be further increases in CY27 capex

IREN from 2024.
What if memory could  consume less of future cloud capex?
Assuming 2027 global cloud capex from major CSPs reaches US$1.5trn, we expect the mix
to remain heavily skewed toward memory and accelerated‑computing infrastructure,
underscoring the increasingly hardware‑intensive nature of AI investment. Memory
represents the largest component at >50% of total spending in our view, reflecting the
growing importance of HBM and other high‑bandwidth memory technologies with high

ASP driven by severe supply shortage.
GPU/ASIC spending excluding HBM accounts for 15–20%, while infrastructure contributes
20%, highlighting substantial investment in data center buildout, power, cooling, and
networking. Although current server CPU exposure remains relatively low, we believe it
will also enjoy rapid growth in the following years given strong agentic AI demand.
However, the current capex mix is heavily influenced by elevated memory pricing. If

memory prices normalize from current levels, we believe CSPs could reallocate a greater
share of budgets toward GPUs/ASICs and server CPUs, allowing the same overall capex
envelope to support more computing capacity. In this scenario, lower memory costs
could effectively improve the purchasing power of cloud capex and potentially accelerate
deployment of incremental AI computing.
